### [1] [Pay Only for Disagreement: Certified No-Regression Verdicts for Model Updates with Matching Label-Complexity Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17560)
*Vishnu Bindu Balachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将模型更新上线（update promotion）形式化为带认证的成对风险差异审计问题，提出DISCERN两级序贯协议：无标签层利用"风险差异只存在于两模型预测不一致的输入上"这一支撑恒等式直接认证良性更新；审计层仅对采样到的不一致样本进行标注，采用任意时刻有效置信序列，并证明有限样本有效性与标签复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的模型会通过重训、微调、量化或供应商静默替换不断更新，每次更新都可能比被替换的版本更差，因此需要在上线前/上线后对更新是否变差进行可认证的审计。传统评估要么需要大量标签，要么忽略"两模型预测一致则对风险差异无贡献"这一可利用的先验结构，导致标注成本高、且难以在多次连续更新中复用同一误差预算。

Method: 核心是支撑恒等式：两模型风险差异只存在于二者预测不一致的输入集合上，且该集合无需标签即可观测。据此设计DISCERN两级序贯协议：零标签层仅用无标签流量，在不一致率低于容差时直接认证良性更新；审计层只对采样到的不一致样本进行标注，并构造任意时刻有效（anytime-valid）的置信序列，使其在所有停机时间和任意标签路由规则（含对抗性判定者）下均有效。作者给出有限样本有效性证明，以及速率层数量级为rho^2/eps^2的匹配标签复杂度界。

Result: 理论结果表明，利用免费的不一致信息可比任何"配对盲"审计器在标签复杂度上证明性地节省1/rho因子，且认证保证可在单一误差预算下跨无界次数的连续上线组合。实证上在785个更新对、14,000+条重放审计流（含最高1.4B参数语言模型的LoRA微调）上，误覆盖率仅0.0002（名义5%），功效0.986且零误报，56%的良性更新实现零标签认证。

Conclusion: DISCERN将更新上线决策转化为可认证的成对风险差异审计，通过利用无标签可观测的不一致结构，在保持有限样本有效性的同时显著降低标注成本，并支持跨多次更新的误差预算组合。每次审计还输出可机器校验的证据记录，可用于上线后监控，为模型更新的持续治理提供了统计上严格且计算可行的方案。

Abstract: Every production model is updated, by retraining, fine-tuning, quantization, or a silent vendor swap, and each update risks being worse than what it replaced. We formalize update promotion as certified paired risk-difference auditing. Our starting point is a support identity: the risk difference between two models lives on the inputs where they disagree, observable without labels. We build DISCERN, a sequential two-tier protocol. A zero-label tier certifies benign updates whose disagreement rate is below tolerance from unlabeled traffic alone. An audited tier labels only sampled disagreements through an anytime-valid confidence sequence, valid at every stopping time and under any label-routing rule, even an adversarial judge. We prove finite-sample validity and matching label-complexity bounds of order rho^2/eps^2 at the rate level, so exploiting free disagreement provably saves a factor 1/rho over any pairing-blind auditor, and the guarantee composes across an unbounded sequence of promotions from one error budget. Across 14,000+ replayed audit streams over 785 update pairs, including LoRA fine-tunes of language models up to 1.4B parameters, miscoverage is 0.0002 (nominal 5%), power 0.986 with zero false alarms, and 56% of benign updates certify with zero labels. Each audit emits a machine-checkable evidence record for post-market monitoring.

</details>


### [2] [Beyond Static RAG: An Adaptive, Tri-Metric Routing Framework for Efficient Long-Context Inference on Commodity GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17564)
*Saipraveen Vabbilisetty,Ajay Kumar Boddepalli,Deep Narayan Mishra,Shashank Kapadia,Haoan Wang,Anupriya Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对在16GB T4等消费级GPU上部署RAG时出现的“压缩悖论”，提出免训练的Tri-Metric Router路由策略，在Raw、Neural（LLMLingua-2）与Lexical（BM25）三种流水线间动态选择，实现0% OOM、88.5±4.4% oracle对齐与49.3% Combined F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在NVIDIA T4（16 GB VRAM）等消费级GPU上部署检索增强生成（RAG）存在实际失效模式：神经提示压缩会引入KV缓存争用与预处理延迟，收益被生成阶段节省所抵消（即“压缩悖论”）；而跳过压缩又会在长上下文下导致OOM。在vLLM服务的LLM与PyTorch压缩器共存于紧张内存预算时，存在两种不同的失效机制，亟需一种能够权衡压缩与内存的调度方案。

Method: 提出Tri-Metric Router：确定性、免训练的策略，在Raw、Neural（LLMLingua-2）与Lexical（BM25）三种流水线间进行选择。使用三个CPU侧信号：空间复杂度L、句法密度ρ_key和类型-词符比（TTR）。与以往仅基于语义的自适应方法不同，其调度信号基于硬件物理特性，即VRAM余量与延迟交叉点。阈值通过LongBench qasper上的profiling标定，在T4上得到约4,332词的运行交叉点；论文强调贡献在于该标定方法论而非特定硬件常量。

Result: 在分布外留出集上，该方法实现0% OOM失败、88.5 ± 4.4% oracle对齐以及49.3% Combined F1，相较始终启用词法压缩提升5.2个百分点，且不增加额外VRAM或训练开销。

Conclusion: Tri-Metric Router通过硬件物理驱动（VRAM余量与延迟交叉点）的确定性、免训练调度，在消费级GPU上有效规避RAG的“压缩悖论”，在不增加显存与训练成本的前提下显著降低OOM并提升F1，其标定方法论具有可迁移性。

Abstract: Deploying retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) on commodity GPUs such as the NVIDIA T4 (16 GB VRAM) exposes a practical failure mode we call the Compression Paradox: neural prompt compression can add key-value (KV) cache contention and preprocessing latency that outweigh generation-time savings, while skipping compression can cause out-of-memory (OOM) failures on long contexts. We identify two distinct failure mechanisms when a vLLM-served LLM and a PyTorch-based compressor are co-deployed under tight memory budgets, and introduce the Tri-Metric Router, a deterministic, training-free policy that selects among Raw, Neural (LLMLingua-2), and Lexical (BM25) pipelines. The router uses three CPU-side signals: spatial complexity ($L$), syntactic density ($ρ_{key}$), and type-token ratio (TTR). Unlike prior semantic-only adaptation, our dispatch signal is hardware-physical, based on VRAM headroom and a latency crossover point. Thresholds are calibrated from profiling on LongBench qasper, yielding an operating crossover near 4,332 words on T4; our contribution is this calibration methodology rather than a hardware-specific constant. On out-of-distribution holdouts, the method achieves 0% OOM failures, 88.5 $\pm$ 4.4% oracle alignment, and 49.3% Combined F1, improving over always-on lexical compression by 5.2 points without additional VRAM or training cost.

</details>


### [3] [Where Grokking Happens: Distributed Utility and Fourier Recoding Without a Module Switch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17571)
*Dekun Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过"Transition Games"精确定位Transformer中从记忆到泛化转变的功能表达：该转变是已有分布式电路的谱重编码，而非模块切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Transformer中从记忆到泛化的转变在何处被功能性地表达这一问题，并检验"MLP负责记忆、注意力负责泛化"这一常见预测。

Method: 提出Transition Games——行为对齐的精确激活博弈，并配以非泛化的配对对照；通过替换博弈分析局部模式贡献，并用独立的精确路径研究考察下游路径的作用。

Result: 发现存在分布式效用增益及前瞻性的block-0注意力偏置；选定的二阶模式在替换博弈中占其加法对比的67–92%；block-1 MLP在其效应中所占比例高于其他所有被测下游路径（12/12对）。而"MLP记忆、注意力泛化"的预测方向相反（记忆锚点处为−0.331 bits/example，0/12符合预测方向），路由起始、全局秩坍塌及素不变架构脊也均失败。

Conclusion: 此处的grokking应被理解为已有分布式电路的谱重编码，而非模块间的功能切换。

Abstract: Where in a Transformer is the change from memorization to generalization functionally expressed? We introduce Transition Games--behavior-aligned exact activation games with paired non-generalizing controls--and find distributed utility gain with a prospective block-0 attention bias; selected degree-two modes account for 67--92% of its addition contrast across replacement games, and a disjoint exact path study confirms that block-1 MLP mediates more of their effect than all other tested downstream paths in 12/12 pairs. The sharper "MLP memorizes, attention generalizes" prediction instead reverses (-.331 bits/example at the memory anchor; 0/12 in the predicted direction), while routing onset, global rank collapse, and a prime-invariant architecture ridge also fail, identifying grokking here as spectral recoding of an existing distributed circuit rather than a module switch.

</details>


### [4] [Disentangling Algorithmic Bias from Archival Artifacts: A Controlled Audit of Vision-Language Model Valuation in Metropolitan Museum Archives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17572)
*Manpreet Singh,Rhythm Bhatia,Rahul Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究审计CLIP模型在艺术品元数据上的社会偏见，发现零样本评分在性别上统计等效，但指出该指标分辨力粗糙，且存在机构生存偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 审计视觉语言模型（VLM）的社会偏见时，需区分算法本身的估值差异与档案元数据中的混杂因素，而现有评估多为单变量、缺乏混杂控制与等效性检验。

Method: 使用大都会艺术博物馆开放获取馆藏历史艺术品元数据（N=1500，署名作品743件：男性534、女性209，匿名618）；构建定量审计框架，评估零样本CLIP在三个语义提示对（masterpiece、quality、influence）上的logit差分分数；进行未调整比较、TOST等效性检验，以及控制媒介、创作年代和长宽比的多元OLS回归，并分析残差嵌入方差。

Result: 未调整评估显示分数高度收敛，OpenAI CLIP（μ_F=-0.0067 vs μ_M=-0.0035，p=0.1829）与OpenCLIP（μ_F=0.0171 vs μ_M=0.0237，p=0.1224）均无显著性别主效应；TOST在Cohen's d≥0.25边界上确认统计等效（pTOST<0.005）。控制媒介、年代、长宽比的OLS回归（R²<0.02）显示艺术家性别无显著条件效应（p>0.20）。残差嵌入方差高（R²<2%），表明全局零样本估值指标接近嵌入噪声下限。

Conclusion: 宏观评分等效反映的是指标对细粒度视觉-语义特征不敏感，并不排除微观局部视觉偏见；排除41.2%未署名藏品带来机构生存偏差。评估文化遗产AI公平性时，必须进行多元混杂控制、等效性检验与档案来源审计。

Abstract: Auditing vision-language models (VLMs) for societal bias requires distinguishing direct algorithmic valuation disparities from confounders embedded within archival metadata. In this study, we audit Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP) models using historical artwork metadata from the Metropolitan Museum of Art Open Access collection (N = 1,500 total objects; N = 743 attributed works: Male n = 534, Female n = 209; n = 618 anonymous).
  We establish a quantitative audit framework evaluating zero-shot CLIP logit differential scores across three semantic prompt pairs (masterpiece, quality, and influence). Unadjusted evaluations demonstrate high score convergence without a statistically significant main gender effect under OpenAI CLIP (mu_F = -0.0067 vs mu_M = -0.0035, p = 0.1829) or OpenCLIP (mu_F = 0.0171 vs mu_M = 0.0237, p = 0.1224). Two One-Sided Tests (TOST) confirm statistical equivalence across Cohen's d >= 0.25 bounds (pTOST < 0.005).
  Multivariate OLS regression controlling for artwork medium, creation era, and aspect ratio (R^2 < 0.02) confirms that artist gender has no statistically significant conditional effect (p > 0.20). High residual embedding variance (R^2 < 2%) indicates that global zero-shot valuation metrics operate near an embedding noise floor, showing that broad zero-shot prompt logit differentials are a coarse measurement instrument rather than proving absolute model fairness.
  We highlight two key caveats: (i) macro-level score equivalence reflects metric insensitivity to fine-grained visual-semantic features and does not preclude localized micro-level visual biases, and (ii) excluding 41.2% unattributed holdings reflects institutional survival bias. These results demonstrate the necessity of multivariate confound control, equivalence testing, and archival provenance auditing when assessing AI fairness in cultural heritage collections.

</details>


### [5] [Temperon: Full-Time SAM Quality at a Third Less Wall-Clock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17575)
*Stamatis Mastromichalakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Temperon：前 43% epoch 用朴素 SGD 探索，剩余全部余弦退火阶段交给 SAM 包裹的 Muon 精修器，通过一次计划性切换，在多个数据集上以更低成本匹配甚至超越全程 SAM 的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM 每步训练开销翻倍，但其收益集中在训练末期。作者据此探究昂贵训练模式应投放在何处，以降低总成本同时保持精度收益。

Method: Temperon 将 epoch 预算划分为阶段：前 43% 使用 plain-SGD 探索，随后一次计划性 hand-off，把整个最终余弦退火阶段分配给 SAM 包裹的 Muon 精修器。并在 CIFAR、SVHN、Tiny ImageNet、GPT-2 预训练与 GLUE 微调上验证。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10/100、SVHN、Tiny ImageNet 上，Temperon 精度全面匹配最佳全程 SAM 配方；四者中三者达到最难常见目标分别快 35%、34%、32%。Muon 精修器带来 +0.85pp 增益，探索器形状与重启无贡献。相比 late-phase SAM，后者中低级目标更快，但 Muon 带来的层级（CIFAR-100 0.83、CIFAR-10 0.97）无任何 SGD 精修方法可达；Tiny ImageNet 上 Muon 未买层级，对手胜出。方法迁移到 GPT-2 预训练（-29% wall-clock）与 GLUE 微调（SAM 成本三分之一）。

Conclusion: 跳过早期 SAM 可买固定信用，Muon epoch 成本为 SAM+SGD epoch 的 1.50x。余弦调度下精度曲线呈平台后骤升，切换时机信息存于调度而非轨迹，因此计划性切换是有原理依据的。代码与可 pip 安装实现已发布。

Abstract: Sharpness-aware minimization (SAM) doubles the cost of every training step, yet its benefit concentrates where training ends. We study where an expensive training mode should be spent and propose Temperon: a plain-SGD explorer for the first 43% of the epoch budget, then one scheduled hand-off that gives the entire final cosine anneal to a SAM-wrapped Muon refiner. On CIFAR-10/100, SVHN and Tiny ImageNet (five seeds, times reported as epochs-to-target times an idle-GPU-calibrated epoch cost), Temperon matches the best full-time-SAM recipe on accuracy everywhere while reaching the hardest common target 35%, 34% and 32% sooner on three of the four, and sits a tier above the published SAM+SGD recipe at level cost. Ablations make the attribution exact: the Muon refiner is worth +0.85pp with everything else fixed; the explorer's shape and its restarts are worth nothing, and we withdraw them as contributions. Re-running the closest rival, late-phase SAM, at matched budget shows the frontier: it is fastest to every mid-level target, but the tier the Muon refiner buys (0.83 on CIFAR-100, 0.97 on CIFAR-10) is reached by no SGD-refined method in any seed, and on Tiny ImageNet, where Muon buys no tier, the rival simply wins -- the measured boundary of the method. The allocation law transfers to GPT-2 pretraining (full-SAM quality at -29% wall-clock) and GLUE fine-tuning (never worse than full-time SAM at a third of its SAM cost). Two constants organize the economics: skipping SAM early buys a fixed credit, and a Muon epoch costs 1.50x a SAM+SGD epoch on all four datasets. Finally, the hand-off cannot be timed from the trajectory: under cosine schedules the accuracy curve is plateau-then-surge, so the information lives in the schedule, making the scheduled switch principled rather than convenient. Code and a pip-installable implementation are released.

</details>


### [6] [When the Gradient Sees Rank: Provable Necessity, Causal Recruitment, and Composition in Trained Matrix Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17594)
*Samuel Larson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究检验基于梯度的训练能否在矩阵记忆中学习到存储与组合关联所需的秩，发现学习到的有效秩随绑定数K增加，秩上限会引发恢复性能的急剧转变，且训练后的算子在多次自应用中保持高恢复率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期研究使用矩阵增强推理器处理仅需rank-1解的任务，无法回答梯度训练是否能学到矩阵记忆所需的更高秩，因此需要设计精确线性恢复要求rank(Z)≥K的K个键值绑定任务来直接检验该问题。

Method: 训练矩阵记忆存储K个新键值绑定，精确线性恢复要求rank(Z)≥K；使用固定线性读出在无权访问原始绑定条件下查询单一矩阵状态；以余弦相似度大于0.9作为恢复判定阈值；在测试网格上测量学习到的有效秩，并施加训练时秩上限，同时在实体子空间分析学习算子与理想循环。

Result: 学习到的有效秩随K增加（d=16时Spearman ρ=1.0）；训练时秩上限导致恢复转变出现在k=K附近（d=8、K=4时，秩3恢复至多0.0004，秩4达0.97）；五个种子中四个在21倍自应用后仍保持至少0.9996恢复；实体子空间上学习算子有效秩接近K并近似理想循环；唯一收敛但秩上限低于K的种子，其预测余弦与实测值在七次应用内误差0.008；延长训练可解决部分初期失败，但在编码器宽度固定时更大矩阵维度下恢复仍下降。

Conclusion: 梯度训练能够学习到与绑定数K相匹配的有效秩，且秩上限会决定恢复成败；训练得到的算子在实体子空间近似理想循环并具备稳定的自应用能力，但恢复性能受矩阵维度与编码器宽度约束。

Abstract: Can gradient-based training learn the rank needed to store and compose associations in a matrix memory? In our earlier study, we used a matrix-augmented reasoner on a task that admits a rank-1 solution, leaving this question open. We train matrix memories on $K$ fresh key-value bindings whose exact linear recovery requires $\mathrm{rank}(Z) \geq K$. A fixed linear readout queries a single matrix state without access to the original bindings. Experiments measure recovery by cosine similarity greater than 0.9, a threshold distinct from mathematical equality. Learned effective rank increases with $K$ across the tested grid (Spearman $ρ= 1.0$ at $d = 16$). Training-time rank caps produce a recovery transition near $k = K$: at $d = 8$, $K = 4$, rank 3 gives at most 0.0004 recovery and rank 4 gives 0.97. Four of five seeds retain at least 0.9996 recovery through 21-fold self-application of the trained operator. On the entity subspace, the learned operator has effective rank close to $K$ and approximates the ideal cycle. For the single converged seed capped below $K$, a calculation using the entity-subspace operator and ideal cycle predicts the measured cosine within 0.008 through seven applications. Extending training resolves several initial failures, but recovery still declines at larger matrix dimensions with encoder width fixed.

</details>


### [7] [Prior-Free Competitive Ratios for Improving Bandits: Scale, Curvature and Horizon Are Free, but Not Jointly Under Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17595)
*Xuan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在改进型多臂老虎机问题中，本文提出探测-承诺（probe-and-commit）算法，去除了已有最优算法的对数因子，达到 Θ(√k+k/T) 的最优竞争比，并在无噪声时实现无需任何先验知识的自适应最优算法，同时刻画了乘法噪声下自适应的代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Blum 与 Ravichandran（ALT 2025）证明在未知尺度 m 时算法存在 O(√k log k) 的近似比，而下界仅为 Ω(√k)，二者间存在不必要的对数因子。此外，现有算法需要知道尺度 m、凹包指数 β 或时间跨度 T 等先验信息。本文旨在消除对数因子、免除先验假设，并量化自适应（不知道先验信息）所需付出的代价。

Method: 提出一页篇幅的探测-承诺（probe-and-commit）算法：先用少量探测估计最优臂的尺度，再集中拉取；并对每个时间跨度确定最优竞争比。无噪声情形采用随机边际探测（random-marginal probing）算法，不读取尺度 m、凹包指数 β 及时间跨度 T。噪声情形在 Blum–Ravichandran 乘法噪声模型下使用嵌套随机排列探测（nested random-permutation probing）算法。

Result: 探测-承诺算法在 T≥2⌊√k⌋ 时达到竞争比 4√3·√k，无需知道尺度；并确定每个时间跨度的最优比为 Θ(√k+k/T)。无噪声时随机边际探测算法对所有 β 和所有时间跨度同时达到最优 Θ(k^{β/(1+β)}+k/T)，无需任何先验。乘法噪声下探测-承诺保持 Θ(√k+k/T)（在相应区间为 Θ(√k)）且不需噪声水平；但自适应代价 φ_ε(k) 为 Θ_ε(√(log k/log log k))，而知道 m 或 β 中任意一个即可将代价降为常数。

Conclusion: 本文通过简洁的探测-承诺算法消除了既有结果中不必要的对数因子，给出了每个时间跨度（甚至未知跨度）下的最优竞争比 Θ(√k+k/T)。无噪声时算法完全无需先验即可对任意 β 与任意跨度同时最优。噪声情形下揭示了自适应的本质困难：同时不知道尺度 m 与凹包指数 β 时，均匀自适应代价为 Θ_ε(√(log k/log log k))，而仅知道其一即可恢复到常数代价，精确刻画了先验信息的价值。

Abstract: In the improving multi-armed bandits problem, each of $k$ arms has an unknown nondecreasing, discretely concave reward curve $f_i$, and pulling arm $i$ for the $t$-th time yields $f_i(t)$. For sufficiently long horizons, Blum and Ravichandran (ALT 2025) proved that randomized algorithms achieve an $O(\sqrt k)$ approximation to the best single arm when the scale $m=f^*(T)$ of the optimal arm is known ($T\ge2k$), and $O(\sqrt k\log k)$ when it is not ($T>4k$), against an $Ω(\sqrt k)$ lower bound. The logarithmic factor is unnecessary: a one-page \emph{probe-and-commit} algorithm achieves competitive ratio $4\sqrt3\,\sqrt k$ for $T\ge2\lfloor\sqrt k\rfloor$, without any knowledge of the scale, and we determine the optimal ratio for every horizon, $Θ(\sqrt k+k/T)$, also for unknown horizons. Without noise, \emph{no prior is needed at all}: a random-marginal probing algorithm reading neither the scale $m$, nor the concavity-envelope exponent $β$ of Blum, Garicano, Ravichandran and Sharma (UAI 2026), nor the horizon $T$, achieves the optimal $Θ(k^{β/(1+β)}+k/T)$ simultaneously for every $β$ and every horizon. Under the multiplicative noise model of Blum and Ravichandran, probe-and-commit keeps the same all-horizon order $Θ(\sqrt k+k/T)$ without knowing the noise level (and $Θ(\sqrt k)$ on the same range), but the price of priors jumps: for any fixed noise level $\varepsilon\in(0,1/2]$, the uniform price of adaptation $φ_\varepsilon(k)$ --- the worst case over horizons $T\ge16k$ of the loss relative to $k^{β/(1+β)}$ for algorithms knowing neither $m$ nor $β$ --- is $Θ_\varepsilon(\sqrt{\log k/\log\log k})$, the lower bound asymptotic in $k$ at fixed positive $\varepsilon$ and matched by a nested random-permutation probing algorithm, whereas knowing either $m$ or $β$ alone restores a constant price.

</details>


### [8] [Lecture notes on Physics Informed Neural Networks, Neural Operators, and their applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17638)
*Alessandro Bombini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文件是博尔扎诺自由大学2025/2026学年“物理信息神经网络”博士课程的讲义，介绍PINN与神经算子（NO）的概念、从零实现的PyTorch代码，以及基于NVIDIA PhysicsNeMo等开源库的工程、物理、油藏等实际问题求解，并涵盖Mixture-of-Models、傅里叶神经算子、PIKANs等近期主题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向博士阶段教学，系统引入物理信息深度学习范式，弥合物理建模与深度神经网络之间的鸿沟，帮助学习者掌握从原理到实现的完整链条，并具备将PINN/NO应用于工程、物理和油藏等真实问题的能力。

Method: 以课程讲义形式组织：先讲授PINN与神经算子的基本概念，随后演示在PyTorch中从零搭建实现，再引入NVIDIA PhysicsNeMo等专用开源库进行进阶与实战；内容涵盖Mixture-of-Models、傅里叶神经算子（FNO）、物理信息Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（PIKANs）等前沿方法。

Result: 形成一套完整的博士课程讲义，覆盖PINN/NO的理论、手工实现与库化实现，并给出面向工程、物理、油藏等领域的应用示例与最新方法介绍。

Conclusion: 该讲义可作为PINN与神经算子方向的教学与入门参考，兼顾理论基础、代码实现与前沿进展，适合希望将物理约束融入深度学习模型的研究者与工程师使用。

Abstract: This is the set of lecture notes for the PhD course \href{https://www.unibz.it/en/faculties/engineering/phd-computer-science/study-course-offering/2025/36967}{\textit{Physics Informed Neural Network}, held at the University of Bozen/Bolzano} in the academic year 2025/2026.
  The goal of the course was to introduce the concept of Physics Informed Deep Neural Networks (PINN) and Neural Operators (NOs), discuss their implementation from scratch in PyTorch and using advanced ad-hoc developed open-source libraries such as NVIDia PhysicsNeMo to address real-world problems in various fields (engineering, physics, petroleum reservoir). We discuss recent topics such as Mixture-of-Models, Fourier Neural Operators, Physics-Informed Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (PIKANs) and Fourier Neural Operators.

</details>


### [9] [Fathom: Per-Query Read Depth for Sparse Decoding over Offloaded KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17652)
*Vivek Kalyanarangan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fathom：以通道分位平面存储4-bit K cache，查询按方差加权重要性反注水分配位预算，实现可变比特键扫描，加速长上下文解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文（百万token）多会话驻留时，KV cache及其索引位于主机内存，对全部n个key进行top-k排序的扫描成为限制解码的瓶颈流量。现有稀疏注意力方法（Double Sparsity、Loki、SparQ）采用固定比特（136-bit或68-bit）读取，无法按查询自适应地在精度与带宽间权衡。

Method: Fathom将4-bit K cache以通道为主序（channel-major）按位平面（bit planes）存储，使t个平面的前缀恰好等价于该通道的t-bit量化器；查询根据各通道方差加权重要性进行反注水（reverse water-filling）分配其比特预算，从而决定每个key通道读取多少比特。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B、百万token下，解码步GPU时间比Double Sparsity、Loki和SparQ r=32的136-bit扫描快1.67倍；在与SparQ 68-bit读取（r=16）相同的GPU时间内，Fathom读取字节数少18%，且在7个模型与上下文设置中有6个注意力误差更低。在RULER风格任务上，每token扫描与精确top-k解码一致；真实编码智能体会话中，Fathom以92-bit达到最精确136-bit扫描的步一致率。

Conclusion: Fathom利用了量化服务栈已持有的4-bit K副本作为存储，无需额外数据结构。其加速来源于主机内存中索引扫描的带宽节省；当索引常驻GPU内存时，该方法不再具有加速优势。

Abstract: When agentic sessions run to a million tokens with many sessions resident at once, the KV cache and the index that ranks it live in host memory, and the scan that ranks all n keys for a top-k step becomes the traffic that bounds decoding. We present Fathom, a key scan in which each query decides how many bits of each key channel to read. The 4-bit K cache is stored channel-major as bit planes, so a prefix of t planes is exactly the channel's t-bit quantizer, and the query spends its bit budget by reverse water-filling over the variance-weighted importance of its channels. At one million tokens on Qwen3-8B a decode step is 1.67x faster in GPU time than with the 136-bit scans of Double Sparsity, Loki and SparQ r=32, and in the same GPU time as SparQ's 68-bit read (r=16) Fathom reads 18% fewer bytes with lower attention error on six of seven model and context settings. On RULER-style tasks every per-token scan matches exact top-k decoding, and on real coding-agent sessions Fathom reaches the step agreement of the most accurate 136-bit scan at 92 bits. The store is the 4-bit K copy a quantized serving stack already holds, and the method is not faster when the index is resident in GPU memory.

</details>


### [10] [On Predicting Post-Click Conversion Rate via Counterfactual Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.04816)
*Junhyung Ahn,Sanghack Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ESCIM（全空间反事实推断多任务模型），以因果推断为原则为非点击样本生成反事实转化标签，缓解CVR预测中点击样本稀疏问题，离线与在线实验均验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CVR预测模型通常仅用点击样本训练（转化只能在点击后确定），但点击样本稀疏，需大量日志才能有效训练。已有利用非点击样本的方法多依赖启发式规则，难以从根本上处理点击与非点击样本间的分布差异与偏差。

Method: 提出ESCIM：先用结构因果模型（SCM）建模用户序列行为；对非点击物品施加假设性干预（即“点击”），推断反事实CVR；再采用多种方法将预测的反事实CVR转化为非点击样本的二元反事实转化标签；最后将生成样本纳入训练，构成多任务学习框架。

Result: 在公开数据集上的大量实验表明所提算法具有优越性；在线A/B测试验证了其在真实场景中的有效性；在潜在转化数据上性能提升，展现出良好的鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: 以因果推断（反事实推断）作为指导原则，为非点击样本生成转化标签并扩充训练数据，可有效缓解CVR预测的样本稀疏与偏差问题，并在离线、在线及潜在转化场景中均取得稳定增益。

Abstract: Accurately predicting conversion rate (CVR) is essential in various recommendation domains such as online advertising systems and e-commerce. These systems utilize user interaction logs, which consist of exposures, clicks, and conversions. CVR prediction models are typically trained solely based on clicked samples, as conversions can only be determined following clicks. However, the sparsity of clicked instances necessitates the collection of a substantial amount of logs for effective model training. Recent works address this issue by devising frameworks that leverage non-clicked samples. While these frameworks aim to reduce biases caused by the discrepancy between clicked and non-clicked samples, they often rely on heuristics. Against this background, we propose a method to counterfactually generate conversion labels for non-clicked samples by using causality as a guiding principle, attempting to answer the question, "Would the user have converted if he or she had clicked the recommended item?" Our approach is named the Entire Space Counterfactual Inference Multi-task Model (ESCIM). We initially train a structural causal model (SCM) of user sequential behaviors and conduct a hypothetical intervention (i.e., click) on non-clicked items to infer counterfactual CVRs. We then introduce several approaches to transform predicted counterfactual CVRs into binary counterfactual conversion labels for the non-clicked samples. Finally, the generated samples are incorporated into the training process. Extensive experiments on public datasets illustrate the superiority of the proposed algorithm. Online A/B testing further empirically validates the effectiveness of our proposed algorithm in real-world scenarios. In addition, we demonstrate the improved performance of the proposed method on latent conversion data, showcasing its robustness and superior generalization capabilities.

</details>


### [11] [Reflect, Revise, Reuse: Training-Free Skill Evolution for GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17653)
*Bofan Chen,Boxuan Zhang,Fei Tang,Zhengxi Lu,Yong Du,Tongbo Chen,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架EvoSkill-GUI，将GUI智能体的技能设计为可在部署期通过执行反馈修订的“活”知识，用“反思-修订-复用”闭环显著提升多基准表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI智能体需在动态界面上执行长时程任务，弹窗、延迟加载与控件位移常使预先固定的计划失效。现有技能框架的技能多为部署前生成的静态产物，未针对GUI执行动态性设计，也无法通过执行过程持续改进。作者认为真正需要的是部署时能依据执行反馈修订、且无需额外训练的技能。

Method: EvoSkill-GUI为免训练框架：每个技能是结构化多文件包，含检索元数据、可执行计划、备用定位、失败恢复规则、无障碍工具与失败案例；运行机制为reflect-revise-reuse闭环——执行器在rollout内即时修订，隔离的critic在严格信息隔离下诊断失败轨迹，执行器通过受限工具接口编辑特定技能文件。

Result: 在MobileWorld、AndroidWorld、OSWorld三个主流GUI基准（覆盖移动端与桌面端）上，EvoSkill-GUI在无训练条件下持续提升多个基座模型，最大增益分别为+16.2%、+6.0%、+10.5%；演化后的技能库能持续支撑相关任务，而非每次从零重建。

Conclusion: GUI智能体需要的是可自执行反馈中迭代演化的技能，EvoSkill-GUI以免训练方式将技能变为动态演化的知识资产，跨平台、跨模型带来稳定增益，并具备技能复用与迁移价值。

Abstract: GUI agents execute long-horizon tasks on dynamic graphical user interfaces, where pop-ups, delayed loads, and relocated widgets routinely invalidate plans fixed before execution. Recent agent-skill frameworks encapsulate reusable procedural knowledge to mitigate this, yet existing skill designs are largely developed without targeting GUI execution dynamics and treat skills as static artifacts produced before deployment rather than living procedural knowledge that improves through it. We argue that what GUI agents need is not better static skills, but skills that can be revised from execution feedback at deployment time, without additional training. We propose \textbf{EvoSkill-GUI}, a training-free framework in which each skill is a structured multi-file package containing retrieval metadata, executable plans, backup localization, failure-recovery rules, accessibility utilities, and failure cases. EvoSkill-GUI operates through a \textbf{\emph{reflect-revise-reuse}} loop: the executor performs instant in-rollout revisions, an isolated critic diagnoses failed trajectories under strict information isolation, and the executor edits specific skill files through a restricted tool interface. Across MobileWorld, AndroidWorld, and OSWorld, three mainstream GUI benchmarks spanning mobile and desktop platforms, EvoSkill-GUI consistently improves multiple base models without any training, with maximum gains of $+16.2\%$, $+6.0\%$, and $+10.5\%$ respectively, and evolved skill libraries continue to benefit related tasks rather than being rebuilt from scratch. Our code is available at https://github.com/ZJU-REAL/EvoSkill-GUI.

</details>


### [12] [Regularized Least Squares Training of Quadratic Neural Networks with Applications to System Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17654)
*Luis Rodrigues,Zachary Yetman Van Egmond,Mohammad R. Amiri Fard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种带正则化的二次神经网络最小二乘训练方法，给出训练优化问题解的下界、近似解的闭式表达及其灵敏度，并将下界计算与核范数最小化建立联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迭代数值方法（如反向传播）计算时间长且可能陷入局部极小值，需要一种能快速求解且具解析表达的训练方法。

Method: 针对带正则化的二次神经网络训练，采用最小二乘途径：在正则化系数为正时推导训练优化问题解的下界，并给出近似解及其对数据误差灵敏度的闭式表达；正则化系数为零时下界紧且近似解即为最优解。

Result: 获得权重的解析表达式、权重对数据误差灵敏度的解析表达式，并建立下界优化与核范数最小化之间的联系；在非线性系统辨识实例中，将所提下界与最优值进行了对比并成功应用。

Conclusion: 闭式权重表达显著降低计算时间，避免陷入局部极小值，所提最小二乘训练方法可有效应用于非线性系统辨识。

Abstract: This paper proposes a least squares approach for the training of quadratic neural networks with regularization. The proposed methodology yields a lower bound on the solution of the training optimization problem for the case where the regularization coefficient is positive. Moreover, it yields closed-form expressions for the approximate solution and its sensitivity The lower bound is tight and the approximate solution is the optimal solution when the regularization coefficient is zero. Having a closed-form expression for the weights reduces considerably the computational time when compared with iterative numerical methods such as backpropagation that can get stuck in local minima. The proposed approach has three main contributions, namely, (i) it yields an analytical expression for the weights, (ii) an analytical expression for the sensitivity of the weights to errors in the data is also provided, (iii) it establishes a connection between the optimization to compute a lower bound and nuclear norm minimization. The proposed least squares training is successfully applied to a nonlinear system identification example where the proposed lower bound is compared with the optimal value.

</details>


### [13] [LIGE-GR: A Smooth Leap from Ranking to Generative Recommendation in the LLM Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18148)
*Venkat Srinivas,Chenzhang He,Sam Woodmansee,Shawn Lian,Wenjie Hu,Renjie Jiang,Ziheng Huang,Xinyuan Zhang,Zhihao Zheng,Zhuoran Yu,Rui Li,Lei Yuan,Ziwei Li,Jimmy Jia,Mert Terzihan,Ekrem Kocaguneli,Yiming Liao,Zhichen Zhao,Yue Yin,Yue Weng,Wanlin Ma,Xufeng Cai,Weimiao Wu,Yezhou Huang,Du Zhang,Yukun Ding,Aaron Johnston,Yueming Wang,Zhaojie Gong,Yuting Zhang,Serena Li,Adithya Ganesh,Boying Liu,Haichuan Yang,Xialu Li,Matt Ma,Qunshu Zhang,John Joshua Miller,Praveen Rathinavelu,Cheng Huang,Aadhar Sachdeva,Josh Karns,Andres Aaron Gutierrez,Neil Agarwal,Gustas Pladis,Vladimir Batygin,Gopal Ray,Aditya Priyadarshi,Shantanu Patil,Zhe Wang,Penny Pan,Yiping Han,Arun Singh,Guangdeng Liao,Bi Xue,Xinyao Hu,Yang Song,Yisong Song,Meihong Wang,Haotian Wu,Deepak Agarwal,Ji Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LIGE-GR，将现有点式推荐系统升级为列表级生成与评估框架，在Instagram Reels和Facebook Video上分别提升用户时长1.14%和0.72%，且仅需少量额外推理资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）的成功为下一代推荐系统提供了启示：推荐与语言生成在结构上都旨在生成优化用户体验的有序序列。然而，如何将LLM范式（尤其是序列级生成与优化）精确融入成熟的工业推荐系统仍是未解问题。两大挑战在于：一是如何把LLM的序列级生成与优化引入推荐；二是现实工业推荐系统历经多年围绕产品、业务约束、服务基础设施和组织归属进行定制迭代，整体替换风险高、组织扰动大。

Method: 提出LIGE-GR（列表级生成与评估推荐框架）。它不重建整个推荐栈，而是将既有的点式（itemwise/pointwise）推荐系统泛化为列表级（listwise）生成系统，从而实现从传统排序系统向生成式推荐范式的升级，同时保持与现有模型、价值函数和服务基础设施的兼容性。

Result: 在Instagram Reels和Facebook Video的短视频推荐场景验证：LIGE-GR使Instagram Reels的用户停留时长提升1.14%，Facebook Video提升0.72%，且仅需适度的额外推理资源。

Conclusion: LIGE-GR表明，成熟工业推荐系统无需推倒重建，即可通过列表级生成与评估获得生成式推荐范式的收益，兼顾列表级优化与现有系统的兼容性，具有较低的落地风险与资源开销。

Abstract: The remarkable success of large language models (LLMs) has provided important inspiration for the next generation of recommender systems. Structurally, recommendation and language generation share a similarity: both aim to produce an ordered sequence that optimizes the user's experience. However, how to precisely absorb the essence of the LLM paradigm into mature industrial recommender systems remains an open problem.
  There are two challenges. First, it is unclear how to incorporate sequence-level generation and optimization from the LLM paradigm into recommendation. Second, real-world recommender systems are mature systems that have been iteratively customized for years around specific products, business constraints, serving infrastructure, and organizational ownership. Replacing such systems wholesale is often technically risky and organizationally disruptive.
  In this paper, we propose LIGE-GR, a listwise generation and evaluation recommendation framework that upgrades from a traditional ranking system based on itemwise recommendation toward a generative recommendation paradigm. Instead of rebuilding the entire recommendation stack from scratch, LIGE-GR generalizes the existing pointwise recommendation system into a listwise generation system. This allows mature recommender systems to benefit from listwise optimization while preserving compatibility with existing models, value functions, and serving infrastructure.
  We validate LIGE-GR in short-video recommendation on Instagram Reels and Facebook Video. On these recommendation surfaces, LIGE-GR improves time spent by 1.14 percent on Instagram Reels and 0.72 percent on Facebook Video, while requiring only modest additional inference resources.

</details>


### [14] [DSD: Learning Diverse and Reusable Motor Skills via Diffusion Skill Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17682)
*Sun Woo Kim,Xue Bin Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Diffusion Skill Discovery（DSD），利用扩散模型通过分数匹配近似策略诱导状态分布的熵梯度，从而在高维人形控制中发现更广、可复用的运动技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类通过学习复用丰富的运动技能库来高效掌握新任务，类似策略也可赋能模拟角色。为支持广泛的下游任务，技能库需兼具多样性与时空变化，而基于互信息最大化的技能发现方法面临高维控制中边际状态熵不可直接估计的难题，现有间接潜空间近似或粗糙估计器难以有效促进状态空间的广泛覆盖，导致技能多样性受限。

Method: 提出DSD方法，使用扩散模型通过分数匹配（score matching）近似策略诱导状态分布的熵梯度，由此构建目标函数，鼓励发现在高维人形控制中产生更广行为范围的技能。学到的技能在下游两种设置中复用：一是带任务特定高层策略的分层控制，二是通过从离线轨迹中进行潜变量选择的零样本控制。

Result: 实验表明，DSD比先前的技能发现方法能发现更广范围的可复用运动技能，并涌现出可跨下游任务复用的复杂且敏捷的行为。

Conclusion: DSD通过扩散模型精确估计熵梯度，有效克服了高维状态空间覆盖不足的问题，实现了更广泛的技能发现与复用，为下游控制任务提供了高质量的技能库。

Abstract: Humans efficiently learn new tasks by reusing a rich repertoire of motor skills across different goals and contexts. A similar strategy can also be used to enable simulated characters to efficiently perform new tasks by leveraging reusable motor skills. To support a wide range of downstream tasks, the learned repertoire should be diverse, consisting of distinct behaviors as well as spatial and temporal variation within each behavior. A commonly used method for learning diverse skills is by maximizing the mutual information between skill latents and the states produced by a policy. The marginal state entropy promotes broad behavioral coverage, while the conditional entropy encourages consistent behaviors from each latent. However, directly estimating the marginal state entropy is intractable in high-dimensional control problems. Prior methods therefore rely on indirect latent-space approximations or coarse estimators of the state distribution. These approximations may not effectively promote broad coverage of the state space, resulting in skills with limited behavioral diversity and reduced utility for downstream tasks. In this work, we propose Diffusion Skill Discovery (DSD), a skill discovery method that uses a diffusion model to approximate the entropy gradient of the policy-induced state distribution through score matching. The resulting objective encourages the discovery of skills that produce a broader range of behaviors for high-dimensional humanoid control. The learned skills are reused in two downstream control settings: hierarchical control with a task-specific high-level policy and zero-shot control through latent selection from offline trajectories. Our experiments show that DSD discovers a broader repertoire of reusable motor skills than prior skill discovery methods, leading to the emergence of complex and agile behaviors that can be reused across downstream tasks.

</details>


### [15] [The Missing "I Don't Know": Why Three Reasoning-Reliability Findings Converge on Calibrated Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17686)
*Srijith Ravikumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 三组近期结果（推理RL破坏工具可靠性表征、安全约束生成下大小模型分别重写/截断被标记片段、一致性推理系统必须无限次幻觉）指向同一缺失能力：校准弃权（calibrated abstention），而主流基准对"拒答"零奖励导致该能力缺乏选择梯度，故提出四项评测改革。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三篇论文分别记录了不同类型的LLM可靠性缺陷，作者希望论证它们并非彼此无关，而是收敛于同一个干预点——校准弃权，从而提出统一的评测改革主张。

Method: 概念性/立场性论证（position paper），无实验：对三组结果进行归因对比，辨别各自"不可用性"来源（能力缺口、政策缺口、递归论缺口），并分析基准奖励结构造成的选择梯度缺失。

Result: 指出诚实性后训练虽缩小了部署模型中的差距，但Bastounis所指问题类的原则性封闭需要校准弃权函数；由于主导基准对"拒绝回答"赋零奖励，选择该函数的排行榜梯度不存在。

Conclusion: 提出四项评测改革：三重评分（triple-scoring）、弃权率报告、能力分层评测、强制校准指标；并强调基准改革是封闭该差距的必要而非充分条件。

Abstract: Three recent results describe what look like unrelated LLM reliability problems. Yin et al. (2026) show reasoning RL collapses tool-reliability representations. Suleymanov et al. (2026) show that under safety-constrained generation, large models rewrite flagged spans while small models truncate. Bastounis et al. (2024) prove any consistent-reasoning system without an implicit "I don't know" function must hallucinate infinitely often on broad problem classes. We argue these findings converge on a single intervention: calibrated abstention is what each independently identifies as the missing capability, even though the unavailability they document, a capability gap, a policy gap, and a recursion-theoretic gap, has a different source in each case. Honesty post-training has narrowed the gap in deployed models, but principled closure of the class Bastounis identifies requires a calibrated abstention function whose training signal at the leaderboard level is absent: dominant benchmarks assign zero reward to decline, so the leaderboard gradient that would select for the function does not exist. We propose four changes to evaluation: triple-scoring, abstention-rate reporting, capability-stratified evaluation, and mandatory calibration metrics. Benchmark reform is necessary, not sufficient, for closing the gap the theorem identifies.

</details>


### [16] [Accelerating Diffusion Sampling via Speculative Draft Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17691)
*Marcello Bullo,Yanxiao Liu,Öykü Sıla Güner,Arpan Mukherjee,Deniz Gündüz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将扩散模型中的投机采样与相对熵编码(REC)建立联系，指出原有反射最大耦合的lookahead受限于链式图结构，提出draft trees扩展候选集，并采用贪婪拒绝采样作为草稿-目标耦合，在保证精确目标分布的同时实现最高8.3%的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于反射最大耦合的扩散采样器存在拓扑约束：其lookahead草稿形成链式图（单一线性序列），这从根本上限制了每次目标函数评估的接受率，导致加速效果受限。

Method: 将扩散模型投机采样与相对熵编码(REC)建立联系，证明lookahead不必是线性的；据此提出draft trees以丰富每轮的候选状态；并采用REC算法中的贪婪拒绝采样(greedy rejection sampling)作为草稿-目标的耦合方式。

Result: 在多种目标模型与草稿模型上的实验表明，相较反射耦合基线，在实际设置下实现最高8.3%的加速。

Conclusion: 通过REC视角解除了lookahead的线性拓扑限制，draft trees与贪婪拒绝采样耦合能够在保证精确目标采样的前提下降低目标函数评估次数，为扩散模型投机采样提供了更高效的新范式。

Abstract: Speculative sampling accelerates diffusion model generation by drafting inexpensive candidate states and correcting them under a coupling that preserves the target distribution exactly, reducing the number of expensive target evaluations. Existing diffusion samplers, notably those based on reflection maximal coupling, are topologically constrained: their lookahead drafts form a chain graph, a single linear sequence, which inherently limits the acceptance rate per target evaluation. We connect speculative sampling in diffusion models to relative entropy coding (REC). This perspective shows the lookahead need not be linear and motivates our central contribution, draft trees, which enrich the candidates considered per round and lower the target function evaluations. We further adopt greedy rejection sampling, an REC algorithm, as the draft-target coupling, improving acceptance while guaranteeing exact target samples. Experiments across diverse target and draft models demonstrate up to 8.3% acceleration over the reflection coupling baseline in practical settings.

</details>


### [17] [Composite-Gradient Learning for Shared Control Authority Between Deep Reinforcement Learning and Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17697)
*Giray Önür,Azita Dabiri,Bart De Schutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出复合梯度学习（CGL）方法，将MPC控制器纳入DRL训练过程，把DRL与MPC的控制输入表示为联合动作并考虑其交互作用；在两类高速公路交通网络上验证，弱交互下收益有限，强交互下部分训练中优于基线但平均增益适中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在DRL-MPC共享控制权的框架中，DRL智能体与MPC控制器各自决定部分控制输入。但常见的学习形式将MPC视为环境的一部分，未显式考虑MPC对控制的贡献及其与DRL智能体的交互，导致训练信号不完整。

Method: 提出复合梯度学习（CGL）方法，将MPC控制器整合进学习过程：把DRL与MPC的控制输入表示为联合动作，并在训练更新DRL智能体时显式考虑二者的交互作用。在具有不同DRL-MPC交互强度的两个多类高速公路交通网络上进行评测，并与将MPC视为环境一部分或仅部分纳入MPC学习的替代方法进行比较。

Result: 在弱交互条件下，CGL带来的收益有限；在强交互条件下，CGL在部分训练运行中学习到性能更高的控制策略，优于替代方法，但平均控制性能增益仍较为有限。

Conclusion: 将MPC显式纳入DRL学习过程（联合动作+交互建模）在强交互场景下具有潜力，但整体性能提升尚不显著，其有效性依赖于DRL与MPC控制输入之间交互的强度。

Abstract: Integrated deep reinforcement learning (DRL) and model predictive control (MPC) methods are increasingly used to control autonomous systems by combining their complementary capabilities. DRL learns control policies through interaction with the environment. MPC uses a system model to optimize control inputs while accounting for constraints. In DRL-MPC frameworks with shared control authority, both the DRL agent and the MPC controller each determine part of the control inputs. However, common learning formulations treat MPC as part of the environment and therefore do not explicitly account for MPC's contribution to control or its interaction with the DRL agent. This paper proposes a novel composite-gradient learning (CGL) method that integrates the MPC controller into the learning process by representing the DRL and MPC control inputs as a joint action and accounting for their interaction when updating the DRL agent during training. CGL is evaluated on two multi-class freeway traffic networks with different strengths of interaction between the DRL and MPC control inputs and it is compared with alternative methods that treat MPC as part of the environment or that only partially incorporate MPC into learning. The results show that CGL offers limited benefit under weak interaction, but learns higher-performing control policies than the alternative methods in a subset of training runs under strong interaction, although the average control-performance gains remain modest.

</details>


### [18] [REVERSAL-BENCH: A Reversibility Axis and Reset Oracle for Measuring the Reset-Free RL Cliff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17745)
*Riyaaz Shaik,Chandru Venkataraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 REVERSAL-BENCH 基准，用连续可逆性参数 ρ 与 reset oracle 检验状态可恢复性，发现无重置智能体在不可逆环境中被永久吸收而学习停滞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主强化学习追求无需外部重置的连续策略训练，但现有范式隐含依赖环境可逆性；真实操作任务（推物坠落、颗粒洒落）不可撤销，导致该假设失效，因此需要一个可控可逆性并可验证状态可恢复性的评测基准。

Method: 构建 REVERSAL-BENCH：通过连续参数 ρ∈[0,1] 控制可逆性，提供 reset oracle 作为真值机制验证状态可恢复性，覆盖五种物理引擎、八种操作设定；评测标准 actor-critic、安全 RL 及专门的无重置框架；并与几何结构相同但可逆的对照环境比较，以因果性地分离不可逆性的影响；另评估一个在不可逆失败前干预的安全盾。

Result: 观察到明显的“可逆性悬崖”：随 ρ 增大，无重置智能体持续被吸收进不可恢复状态，而分幕式（episodic）智能体保持稳定学习；该失效在无重置基线与约束 RL 中均出现，并在学习到的操作策略下的完整物理仿真中持续存在；与几何相同的可逆对照对比确认崩溃由不可逆性而非障碍复杂度导致。安全盾实验表明可恢复性可被准确预测，但主动恢复仅在智能体能物理上避开陷阱时才主要成功。

Conclusion: 不可逆性是导致无重置强化学习失效的因果瓶颈，一次进入不可恢复状态即造成永久吸收并终止学习；仅靠预测可恢复性不足以保证主动恢复，需结合物理上可行的规避。作者发布基准套件、带可恢复性标注的多模拟器数据集与 reset oracle，为可逆性感知的自主 RL 研究提供平台。

Abstract: A central goal of autonomous reinforcement learning is continuous policy training without external resets. However, existing paradigms largely depend on underlying environmental reversibility, a property absent in real world manipulation, where events such as pushing objects off tables or spilling granular substances cannot be undone. We introduce REVERSAL-BENCH, a benchmark that controls reversibility via a continuous parameter $ρ\in [0, 1]$ and provides a reset oracle, a ground-truth verification mechanism to test state recoverability across eight manipulation settings in five physics engines. Evaluating a broad spectrum of policy architectures, including standard actor-critic algorithms, safe RL, and specialized reset-free frameworks, reveals a sharp reversibility cliff: reset-free agents are consistently absorbed into irrecoverable states as $ρ$ increases, whereas episodic agents maintain steady learning. We see this failure mode across autonomous reset-free baselines and constrained RL. Because reset-free agents lack external resets, any transition into an irrecoverable state results in permanent absorption, leaving the agent trapped where further learning halts. We show that this absorption phenomenon persists in full physics simulations under learned manipulation policies. By evaluating against geometrically identical reversible counterparts, we confirm that this breakdown is causally driven by irreversibility rather than obstacle complexity. We release the benchmark suite, a large multi-simulator dataset labeled with recoverability and a reset oracle. We also evaluate a safety shield that intervenes before irreversible failures occur, showing that while recoverability can be predicted accurately, active recovery primarily succeeds only when the agent can physically steer clear of the trap

</details>


### [19] [SAM-on-the-Curve: Sharpness-Aware Mode Connectivity for Robust Weight-Space Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17748)
*Alejandro Calatrava,Xu Zhang,Ren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将模式连通性（MC）重新表述为邻域鲁棒路径优化问题，提出尖锐感知模式连通性（SMC），使整条路径邻域保持低损失，在损坏数据上显著优于标准MC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模式连通性仅约束一维轨迹上的损失，未约束路径周围的权重空间邻域，优化得到的曲线可能穿越尖锐脊线，在分布偏移下变得脆弱。作者认为低损失连通性是不完整的几何判据。

Method: 将模式连通性重构为邻域鲁棒路径优化问题，寻找整条局部邻域均保持低损失的曲线；提出SMC，对所得minimax泛函施加一阶尖锐感知近似，使平坦性沿整条曲线成立而非仅曲线上成立；并推导该尖锐感知目标下连通路径的实际优化算法。

Result: 在CIFAR-10-C严重模糊损坏下，SMC相较标准MC最高取得6.09%的绝对准确率提升；SMC产生负损失障碍，即路径内点所得模型可优于端点平均损失。结果在CIFAR-10与ImageNet-100上，跨ResNet-18、VGG16-BN和ViT-Tiny得到验证。

Conclusion: 路径级平坦性可作为鲁棒权重空间插值的实用原则，弥补了传统模式连通性仅关注一维轨迹低损失的不足。

Abstract: Deep neural networks that are independently trained to similar performance can be connected by low-loss parametric curves in weight space, a phenomenon known as Mode Connectivity (MC). This geometric property underpins practical techniques such as weight averaging, model ensembling, and model merging. We argue that low-loss connectivity is an incomplete geometric criterion: it controls loss only along a one-dimensional trajectory while leaving the surrounding weight-space neighborhood unconstrained, so the optimized curve may traverse sharp ridges that become fragile under distribution shift. We therefore reformulate mode connectivity as a neighborhood-robust path optimization problem, seeking a curve whose entire local neighborhood maintains low loss. We propose Sharp Mode Connectivity (SMC), which applies a first-order sharpness-aware approximation to the resulting minimax functional, enforcing flatness along the entire curve rather than only on it. We derive a practical optimization algorithm for connectivity paths under this sharpness-aware objective. Under severe blur corruptions from CIFAR-10-C, SMC achieves up to 6.09\% absolute accuracy improvement over standard MC. Remarkably, SMC produces negative loss barriers, meaning that models obtained at interior points of the optimized path can outperform the average endpoint loss. These results, validated across ResNet-18, VGG16-BN, and ViT-Tiny on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet-100, establish path-wise flatness as a practical principle for robust weight-space interpolation.

</details>


### [20] [Modular Deep Learning Mechanisms for Auditable Next-Day Wildfire Spread Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17763)
*Miguel Esparza,Aydin Ayanzadeh Ahmad Mousavi,Ali Mostafavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究在次日野火预测中引入三种模块化增强（风与坡度条件注意力偏置、物理特征检索增强输出校正、火情条件双流门控），在Next Day Wildfire Spread基准上以五种骨干网络进行分阶段消融与物理性审计，结果表明预测性能、可操作可信度与计算实用性可以兼顾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 次日野火预测不仅要求预测精度，还需要模型预测能与计算中使用的假设及历史证据一并被评估。深度学习虽可从遥感数据学习空间模式，但仅有预测性能无法确立物理保真度或可操作可信度，因此需要可检验的领域知识组件。

Method: 提出三种模块化增强：1）风与坡度条件注意力偏置，显式暴露预设方向偏好；2）物理特征检索增强输出校正，用九维环境与火状态描述子检索历史瓦片，并对冻结模型logits施加学习式校正；3）火情条件双流门控。在Next Day Wildfire Spread基准上跨五种骨干网络评估，采用分阶段消融、方向性审计、检索扰动、校准度量与计算开销比较。

Result: SwinUNETR在三项增强下三随机种子平均F1为0.4216、AUC-PR为0.3673；混合集成（两种增强架构与一种非增强架构）达到0.4292与0.3790。增益随架构而异，检索带来的AUC-PR提升未一致转化为F1提升。构造的风偏置与输入风高度一致，但与观测到的次日火位移对齐明显较弱。

Conclusion: 研究提供了一个在野火预测模型中暴露并评估特定领域知识组件的框架，并区分了先验可检验性与预测性物理保真度；结果表明预测性能、可操作可信度与计算实用性并非相互竞争的目标。

Abstract: Next-day wildfire prediction requires models whose forecasts can be evaluated alongside the assumptions and historical evidence used in their computation. Although deep learning can learn spatial patterns from remote-sensing data, predictive performance alone does not establish physical fidelity or operational trustworthiness. This study investigates three modular augmentations for next-day active-fire prediction: wind- and slope-conditioned attention biases, physics-feature retrieval-augmented output correction, and fire conditioned dual-stream gating. The attention biases expose prescribed directional preferences, while the retrieval module selects historical tiles using a nine-dimensional environmental and fire-state descriptor and applies a learned correction to a frozen model's logits. The modules are evaluated across five backbones on the Next Day Wildfire Spread benchmark, using staged ablations, directional audits, retrieval perturbations, calibration measures, and computational comparisons. The three-seed mean F1 score and area under the precision--recall curve (AUC-PR) of a SwinUNETR model with all three augmentations are 0.4216 and 0.3673. Then, a mixed ensemble (two augmented architectures and one non-augmented architecture) model achieves 0.4292 and 0.3790. Benefits vary across architectures, and retrieval-related improvements in AUC-PR do not consistently translate into higher F1. The constructed wind bias aligns closely with input wind, but its alignment with observed next-day fire displacement is much weaker, distinguishing prior inspectability from predictive physical fidelity. The study contributes a framework for exposing and evaluating selected domain-informed components within wildfire prediction models. Together, the results presented show that predictive performance, operational trustworthiness, and computational practicality need not be competing objectives.

</details>


### [21] [Principled Koopman Representations with Kalman Inference for Efficient Time-Series Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17815)
*Ruiquan Li,Yuheng Bu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: K$^2$SVD通过优化Hilbert-Schmidt目标显式学习Koopman算子的主导奇异函数，构建低秩Koopman空间，并结合线性高斯状态空间模型与卡尔曼滤波进行多步预测，性能优于现有方法且更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于神经网络学习潜在Koopman空间的工作往往无法构建有效的Koopman空间用于预测：这些表示在数学上与算子理论 formulation 不一致，且未能捕捉系统动态的内在低秩结构。

Method: K$^2$SVD通过优化Hilbert-Schmidt目标，显式学习Koopman算子的主导奇异函数，从而得到具有可解释线性组合的良定义低秩Koopman算子近似；在学到的Koopman空间中，采用线性高斯状态空间模型刻画时间演化，并通过卡尔曼滤波进行推断，以缓解多步预测中的噪声累积。

Result: 在多个数据集上，K$^2$SVD优于当前最先进方法，预测速度显著更快，计算成本低于以往以效率为重点的模型；学习到的潜在空间维度不到先前工作的10%。

Conclusion: 该工作表明有原则的低秩Koopman表示具有显著优势，并为更广泛的应用开辟了潜力。

Abstract: The Koopman operator has been widely used for time-series prediction in dynamical systems. However, prior work that learns latent ``Koopman spaces'' using neural networks often did not construct a valid Koopman space for forecasting, as these representations may be mathematically inconsistent with the operator-theoretic formulation and fail to capture the intrinsic low-rank structure of system dynamics. To address this issue, we introduce K$^2$SVD, a method that explicitly learns the leading singular functions of the Koopman operator by optimizing a Hilbert-Schmidt objective. This yields a well-defined low-rank approximation of the Koopman operator with an interpretable linear combination, featuring a compact latent space with less than $10\%$ of the dimensions used in previous work. In the learned Koopman space, K$^2$SVD further captures temporal evolution with a linear Gaussian state-space model and performs inference via Kalman filtering, mitigating noise accumulation during multi-step prediction. Empirical results show that K$^2$SVD outperforms state-of-the-art methods across multiple datasets, with significantly faster prediction speeds and lower computational cost than previous efficiency-focused models. This highlights the benefits of principled low-rank Koopman representations and opens up broader potential for applications.

</details>


### [22] [The Free Inference Dimension: Complexity Measure for Zero-Collision Navigation under Hypothesis Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17816)
*Luiz Carlos Castro Guedes,Edward Hermann Haeusler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究嵌套约束族下的有限元强化学习，提出"自由推断"现象：值混合（VM）智能体可在不识别真实环境的情况下实现近最优、零碰撞导航，直至达到密度阈值，之后后验模式选择（PMS）更优。作者用组合度量 dFI 刻画该现象，并给出泛化界与最优混合策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Solomonoff归纳将预测视为可计算假设的混合，通常导致识别真实环境。但在有限元强化学习、嵌套约束族的设定中，先前工作观察到不同机制：VM智能体无需识别真实环境即可达到近最优零碰撞导航（自由推断）。该机制在密度阈值处失效，性能下降且PMS更优，因此需要形式化刻画其能力边界与失效条件。

Method: 1) 定义自由推断维度 dFI(S,N)，以组合方式度量VM智能体在保持轨迹连贯性时可处理的环境复杂度；2) 证明 dFI 严格小于VC维，并以路径长度因子与Natarajan维相关，刻画不可分解损失的代价；3) 给出PAC风格松弛，导出由 dFI^(ε,δ) 驱动的泛化界；4) 定义互补的PMS识别维度；5) 分析并证明"先平均、首次碰撞后切换选择"的混合策略最优性，并与Littlestone型维度建立联系；6) 用栅格世界实验验证理论。

Result: 理论上得到：dFI严格小于VC维，且与Natarajan维相差路径长度因子；PAC松弛给出以dFI^(ε,δ)为量的泛化界；混合策略（平均至首次碰撞再切换为选择）被证明是最优的，并与Littlestone型维度相关联。实验中栅格世界结果支持上述理论预测。

Conclusion: 自由推断是一种在特定密度阈值内可维持的、无需识别真实环境即可实现近最优导航的机制，其能力由组合维度 dFI 精确界定；超过阈值后应转向PMS。最优策略为"先平均后选择"的混合方案，该框架将VM与PMS统一，并与经典维度理论（VC、Natarajan、Littlestone）建立联系。

Abstract: Solomonoff induction frames prediction as a mixture over computable hypotheses, typically leading to identification of the true environment. In a finite meta-reinforcement learning setting with nested constraint families, in our previous work, we observe a different regime: a value-mixture (VM) agent achieves near-optimal, zero-collision navigation without identifying the true environment, a phenomenon we call Free Inference. This regime persists up to a sharp density threshold, beyond which performance degrades and posterior-mode selection (PMS) becomes preferable.
  We formalize this behavior via the Free Inference dimension dFI(S,N), a combinatorial measure of the environmental complexity a VM agent can handle while preserving trajectory coherence. We prove dFI is strictly smaller than the VC-dimension and relates to the Natarajan dimension up to a path-length factor, capturing the cost of non-decomposable loss. A PAC-style relaxation yields generalization bounds driven by dFI^(epsilon,delta). We also define a complementary PMS identification dimension and show that a hybrid strategy---averaging until the first collision, then switching to selection---is optimal, with links to Littlestone-type dimensions supported by grid-world experiments.

</details>


### [23] [NObSP: Functional Decomposition of Neural Networks via Oblique Subspace Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17825)
*Alexander Caicedo,Víctor De La Hoz,Santiago Alférez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 NObSP 框架，利用训练网络线性末层与样本空间斜投影，将预测分解为逐特征贡献函数与交互残差，并给出 NObSP-CAM 实现，在忠实度与功能再现上优于 KernelSHAP、Integrated Gradients。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络决策机制难以解释，现有归因方法多输出标量重要性，难以刻画非线性特征贡献与交互，且学习到的特征子空间重叠时易出现双重计数问题。

Method: NObSP 利用网络线性末层，在样本空间使用斜投影（oblique projections）分解预测为逐特征贡献函数与交互残差，减少子空间重叠导致的双重计数；建立与 functional ANOVA 及 Kolmogorov-Arnold 表示定理的联系，推导高效偏回归算法用于样本外评估；针对卷积网络提出 NObSP-CAM，经一次性校准后无需反向传播即可生成类激活图。

Result: 在表格与视觉基准上忠实度与主流归因方法相当；在已知分量函数的合成基准上，函数再现分数（Function Reproduction Score）达 0.989，高于 KernelSHAP 的 0.966 与 Integrated Gradients 的 0.922；在 TinyImageNet 上，贡献向量嵌入将平均最近邻类纯度从原始激活的 0.654 提升至 0.713，平均近邻距离减少一半以上。

Conclusion: NObSP 通过恢复具有可分离正负证据的功能贡献轮廓，补充了标量归因方法，兼具局部解释与全局功能分析能力。

Abstract: Understanding how deep neural networks make decisions remains a fundamental challenge. We present NObSP (Nonlinear Oblique Subspace Projections), a framework that decomposes predictions into explicit per feature contribution functions and an interaction residual. NObSP exploits the linear final layer of a trained network and uses oblique projections in sample space to reduce double counting when learned feature subspaces overlap, thereby supporting both local explanations and global functional analysis. We establish connections to functional ANOVA and the Kolmogorov-Arnold representation theorem and derive an efficient partial regression algorithm for out of sample evaluation. For convolutional networks, NObSP-CAM produces class activation maps without backward passes after a one time calibration. Experiments on tabular and vision benchmarks show faithfulness comparable to established attribution methods. On a synthetic benchmark with known component functions, NObSP obtains a Function Reproduction Score of 0.989, compared with 0.966 for KernelSHAP and 0.922 for Integrated Gradients. On TinyImageNet, contribution vector embeddings improve mean nearest neighbor class purity from 0.654 for raw activations to 0.713 and reduce mean neighbor distance by more than half. These results indicate that NObSP complements scalar attribution methods by recovering functional contribution profiles with separable positive and negative evidence.

</details>


### [24] [Procedural Pretraining for Molecular Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17831)
*Moritz Friedemann,Zachary Shinnick,Philip Torr,Bruno Andreis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"程序化预训练"三阶段流水线（程序化预训练→SMILES分子预训练→下游微调），发现程序化任务（序列结构、元胞自动机、图推理）可在分子预训练之后仍提升分子性质预测，且转移效应在数据稀缺时最强、非单调、集中于注意力层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子性质预测受限于下游标注数据规模小，通常依赖大规模无标注分子语料预训练。作者追问：在模型接触任何分子数据之前，能否从抽象、程序生成的合成数据中学习到有用的归纳偏置，从而为分子学习提供一种补充性迁移来源。

Method: 三阶段训练流水线：程序化预训练（procedural pretraining）、基于SMILES的分子预训练、下游微调。评估了覆盖序列结构、元胞自动机与图推理的多种程序化任务。通过消融分析考察下游数据稀缺程度、程序化数据结构（而非仅表面统计量）、程序化训练预算的影响，并定位可迁移信息在注意力层与前馈层中的分布。

Result: 程序化预训练在后续分子预训练之后仍能提升分子性质预测：在Lipophilicity上Reverse使测试误差降低4.8%，约为250K分子基线与此前公开的、在约100M分子上预训练的MoLFormer检查点之间性能差距的90%。收益在下游数据稀缺时最强；依赖程序化数据的结构而非仅表面统计；不随程序化训练量单调增长，通常在中等预算处峰值，接近程序化任务收敛时反而恶化。多个任务中可迁移信息主要集中于注意力层，前馈层可能导致过度特化。

Conclusion: 程序化数据能为分子学习提供可迁移的结构，在标注分子数据有限时构成一条互补的性能提升路径。

Abstract: Molecular property prediction is often limited by the small size of labeled downstream datasets, motivating pretraining on large corpora of unlabeled molecules. In this work, we ask whether useful inductive biases can instead be learned from abstract, procedurally generated data before a model sees any molecular data. We introduce a three-stage training pipeline consisting of procedural pretraining, molecular pretraining on SMILES, and downstream fine-tuning, and evaluate several procedural tasks spanning sequence structure, cellular automata, and graph reasoning. We find that procedural pretraining can improve molecular property prediction even after subsequent molecular pretraining: on Lipophilicity, \textsc{Reverse} reduces test error by 4.8\%. For context, the magnitude of this improvement is roughly 90\% of the performance difference between our 250K-molecule baseline and the publicly released MoLFormer checkpoint pretrained on approximately 100M molecules. Our analysis shows that the benefit is strongest under downstream data scarcity, depends on the structure of the procedural data rather than only surface-level statistics, and does not increase monotonically with additional procedural training. Instead, transfer typically peaks at an intermediate procedural budget and deteriorates as the model approaches convergence on the procedural task. We further find that, for several tasks, much of the transferable information is localized in the attention layers, while feed-forward layers can contribute to over-specialization. These results show that procedural data can provide transferable structure for molecular learning and offer a complementary route to improving performance when labeled molecular data are limited.

</details>


### [25] [Adaptive hybrid coupling with operator inference, the overlapping Schwarz alternating method and reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17837)
*Trishit Mondal,Irina Tezaur,Anthony Gruber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于强化学习（DQN）的在线自适应方法，动态调整重叠Schwarz交替法耦合的FOM/ROM子域模型分配，在移动前沿与弹性波传播问题中优于静态分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合域分解方法可耦合全阶模型（FOM）与降阶模型（ROM），但通常假设每个子域的模型分配在仿真过程中固定不变。这对局部特征随时间传播、需高保真分辨率的区域动态变化的瞬态问题构成限制。

Method: 将FOM-ROM耦合于重叠Schwarz交替法（O-SAM）框架，通过重叠界面上的传输边界条件交换解信息并迭代求解子域局部问题。离线训练深度Q网络（DQN），使其在各子域的FOM与预训练Operator Inference（OpInf）ROM之间进行选择，奖励函数同时权衡精度、计算成本与模型切换频率。训练后的策略可在未见过的算例上预测性部署，无需参考FOM解。

Result: 在两个算例上验证：（1）含移动前沿的1D对流扩散问题，学习策略随前沿传播动态分配高保真分辨率，性能优于静态FOM/ROM分配；同时让智能体自适应域分解未带来额外收益。（2）基于Norma.jl固体力学代码的3D线弹性波传播问题，两子域与三子域分解下的学习策略均能跟踪传播波前，将FOM分配给包含波的子域、其余子域使用ROM，符合预期。

Conclusion: 结果表明强化学习在Schwarz类混合仿真中实现模型保真度的预测性在线自适应具有潜力。

Abstract: Hybrid domain decomposition methods provide a flexible framework for coupling full order models (FOMs) and reduced order models (ROMs), but typically assume the model assigned to each subdomain is fixed throughout a simulation. This is limiting for transient problems in which localized features propagate through the domain and the regions requiring high-fidelity resolution change over time. We introduce a reinforcement learning (RL)-based approach for online adaptation of FOM-ROM models coupled via the overlapping Schwarz alternating method (O-SAM), an iterative domain decomposition method that solves subdomain-local problems while exchanging solution information through transmission boundary conditions on overlapping interfaces. Deep Q-networks (DQNs) are trained offline to select among subdomain-local FOMs and pre-trained Operator Inference (OpInf) ROMs using a reward balancing accuracy, cost, and model-switching frequency. Once trained, the policies are deployed predictively on problem instances not seen during training, without requiring a reference FOM solution. We demonstrate the approach on two examples: a 1D advection-diffusion problem with a moving front, and a 3D linear elastic wave propagation problem implemented in the Norma.jl solid mechanics code. For the advection-diffusion benchmark, the learned policy dynamically allocates high-fidelity resolution as the front propagates and outperforms static FOM/ROM assignments; letting the agent also adapt the domain decomposition provides no further benefit. For the elastic wave benchmark, learned policies for two and three subdomain decompositions track the propagating wave by assigning FOMs to subdomains containing the wave and ROMs elsewhere, as expected. Our results demonstrate the potential of RL to enable predictive online adaptation of model fidelity within Schwarz-based hybrid simulations.

</details>


### [26] [Hybrid coupling with numerics-informed neural networks and the overlapping Schwarz alternating method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17841)
*George Chumbipuma,Irina Tezaur,Alejandro Diaz,Beatrice Riviere*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合建模框架，利用重叠Schwarz交替法将预训练数值信息神经网络（NINN）与经典全阶模型（FOM）耦合，在Peclet数10^6的二维对流扩散问题上验证了NINN单体训练与NINN-FOM混合求解的可行性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息神经网络（PINN）在强对流（高Peclet数）问题中难以稳定训练，作者希望借助数值信息神经网络（NINN）与经典数值模型耦合，兼顾神经网络建模能力与FOM的可靠性，并探索无需全域求解的部署方式。

Method: 采用重叠乘性Schwarz迭代作为耦合机制，将预训练的子域局部NINN与相邻FOM耦合，NINN权重在Schwarz迭代中固定不变；对比两种NINN训练策略：自顶向下（边界数据来自全域FOM-FOM Schwarz求解）和自底向上（在NINN子域上合成边界轨迹，无需全域求解）。

Result: 与对应PINN不同，单体NINN无需域分解即可在该问题上准确训练；混合NINN-FOM解与FOM-FOM Schwarz解高度一致，自顶向下与自底向上训练策略精度相当。

Conclusion: 重叠Schwarz交替法可有效作为预训练NINN与FOM的耦合部署机制，且自底向上训练策略无需全域求解即可获得与自顶向下相当的精度，为高Peclet数问题提供了一种可行的混合建模途径。

Abstract: We develop a hybrid modeling framework for coupling pre-trained numerics-informed neural networks (NINNs) with classical full order models (FOMs) using the overlapping Schwarz alternating method. We consider the two-dimensional advection-diffusion equation in the advection-dominated, Peclet-number 10^6 regime. We first demonstrate that, unlike the corresponding physics-informed neural network (PINN), a monolithic NINN can be accurately trained on our model problem without domain decomposition. We then employ overlapping multiplicative Schwarz as a deployment mechanism for coupling a pre-trained, subdomain-local NINN with a neighboring FOM, with the NINN weights held fixed throughout the Schwarz iteration. We consider two training approaches for the subdomain-local NINNs: a top-down approach, in which boundary data are obtained from a coupled Schwarz solve on the full domain with a FOM on each subdomain (FOM-FOM Schwarz), and a bottom-up approach, in which boundary traces are generated synthetically on the NINN subdomain without requiring any full-domain solves. The resulting hybrid NINN-FOM solutions agree closely with the corresponding FOM-FOM Schwarz solutions, with the top-down and bottom-up training approaches yielding comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [27] [Uncertainty-Aware Continual Learning for Open-World Intent Discovery Under an evolving Label Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17866)
*Pisante Aida,Formentin Simone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统一的不确定性感知概率框架，用于演化标签空间下的持续新意图发现，通过自适应β-VAE、多信号决策、密度聚类与重放/EWC实现受控标签扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实智能系统运行于开放世界，用户意图并非先验固定或可穷举，且会随新交互模式演化，因此需要在演化标签空间下持续发现新意图。

Method: 以自适应β-VAE编码话语为隐均值，用于分类与密度建模，并输出后验不确定性作为全局可靠性信号；融合分类器置信度、后验不确定性与DP-GMM似然的多信号决策区分已知与潜在新意图；候选新样本经密度聚类发现模块聚类，仅可靠簇提升为新标签以受控扩展标签空间；结合重放与弹性权重巩固缓解灾难性遗忘。

Result: 实验显示高新颖性精度、跨序列阶段稳定适应及有限遗忘；NMI与ARI接近零，表明无法重建完整细粒度意图分类体系，符合框架保守提升策略；定性分析显示密集且局部连贯的语义簇，说明可发现可靠新结构但无需穷尽恢复底层分类体系。

Conclusion: 该工作将持续意图发现形式化为结构化多阶段开放世界问题，提出稳定性—可塑性约束下的自适应标签空间扩展，并以后验不确定性调控可信样本选择、伪标注、新颖性准入与重放；在保守提升策略下可实现可靠新意图结构的局部发现。

Abstract: Real-world intelligent systems increasingly operate under open-world conditions, where user intents are not fixed or exhaustively known a priori and may evolve as new interaction patterns emerge. This paper proposes a unified uncertainty-aware probabilistic framework for continual new intent discovery under an evolving label space. Each utterance is encoded through an adaptive $β$-VAE into a latent mean, used for classification and density modelling and a posterior uncertainty estimate acting as a global reliability signal. Classifier confidence, posterior uncertainty and DP-GMM likelihood are combined through a multi-signal decision mechanism to distinguish known intents from potentially novel samples. Candidate novel instances are clustered through a density-based discovery module and only reliable clusters are promoted to new labels, enabling controlled label-space expansion. Replay and Elastic Weight Consolidation mitigate catastrophic forgetting and preserve previously acquired knowledge. The paper formalises continual intent discovery as a structured multi-phase open-world problem, introduces adaptive label-space expansion under stability--plasticity constraints and uses posterior uncertainty to regulate trusted-sample selection, pseudo-labelling, novelty admission and replay. Experiments show high novelty precision, stable adaptation across sequential phases and limited forgetting. Near-zero NMI and ARI indicate limited reconstruction of the complete fine-grained intent taxonomy, consistent with the framework's conservative promotion strategy. Qualitative analyses nevertheless reveal dense and locally coherent semantic clusters, showing that reliable novel structures can be discovered without exhaustive recovery of the underlying taxonomy.

</details>


### [28] [Dataset-Dependent Effects of Cross-Depth Aggregation and Soft-Routed Experts in EEG Foundation Model Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17886)
*Mingyang Jiang,Yamin Li,Daniel Moyer,Fan Ma,Hua Xu,Catie Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过在CBraMod中引入跨深度注意力残差(AttnRes)与两个软路由专家库，验证专用模块能否提升全微调EEG基础模型。结果显示增益高度依赖数据集且偶有相反效应，同时带来2.4-3.0倍运行时与1.9-2.9倍内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG解码既依赖时间动态也依赖跨通道关系，作者希望检验针对这两种特性设计的专用模块，能否在全微调的EEG基础模型（CBraMod）之上带来一致提升。

Method: 以全微调CBraMod为基线，加入跨深度注意力残差(AttnRes)模块与两个软路由专家库；在FACED、ISRUC、SEED-V、PhysioNet-MI四个数据集上进行三随机种子匹配实验，并对比AttnRes单独使用与叠加专家的效果，同时统计运行时与显存开销。

Result: 完整模型相对全微调的平衡准确率变化分别为-0.12、+1.27、+0.77、-1.27个百分点。AttnRes单独在三个数据集上提升平均平衡准确率；在AttnRes之上叠加专家仅对FACED和SEED-V有益。开销方面：AttnRes为2.11-2.88倍运行时、1.78-2.67倍内存；完整模型为2.41-3.04倍运行时、1.86-2.85倍内存。

Conclusion: 所添加的专用模块带来的是依赖数据集、有时相互矛盾的效应，而非相对全微调的稳定收益，且伴随显著的计算与内存开销。

Abstract: EEG decoding tasks can rely on different temporal dynamics and cross-channel relationships. We test whether specialized modules improve a fully fine-tuned EEG foundation model by augmenting CBraMod with cross-depth Attention Residuals (AttnRes) and two soft-routed expert banks. Across matched three-seed experiments on FACED, ISRUC, SEED-V, and PhysioNet-MI, the complete model changes mean balanced accuracy relative to full fine-tuning by -0.12, +1.27, +0.77, and -1.27 points, respectively. AttnRes alone improves mean balanced accuracy on three datasets, whereas adding experts on top of AttnRes helps only FACED and SEED-V. These gains come with substantial overhead: AttnRes requires 2.11 to 2.88x runtime and 1.78 to 2.67x memory, while the complete model requires 2.41 to 3.04x runtime and 1.86 to 2.85x memory. Overall, the added modules produce dataset-dependent, sometimes opposing effects rather than consistent gains over full fine-tuning.

</details>


### [29] [Long-Context Demonstration Selection Using State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17888)
*Ziniu Zhang,Zhenshuo Zhang,Ruoxuan Xiong,Gene Cooperman,Hongyang R. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究长上下文场景下的示例选择（demonstration selection）问题，提出基于状态空间模型（SSM）的两阶段方法：先通过蒸馏将Transformer各连续层分组、为每组拟合独立SSM以复现层间输入输出行为，再将蒸馏输出映射为少量token用于下游示例选择。实验表明蒸馏SSM相对真实输出的近似误差低于0.7%，并在文本分类与推理任务上较基线降低14.2倍FLOPs、提升6.48%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 示例选择与上下文学习及语言模型推理密切相关。由于Transformer推理成本随序列长度呈二次增长，在长上下文场景中示例选择的计算开销尤为突出，亟需一种在保持效果的同时降低推理成本的方法。

Method: 方法基于状态空间模型（SSM，具有线性推理时间），包含两个算法：(1) 通过对已训练Transformer进行蒸馏学习一小组SSM——将所有层划分为连续层组，对每组估计一个独立的SSM以复制相邻层间的输入输出行为；(2) 将蒸馏模型输出映射到少量token，并利用这些嵌入在下游应用中完成示例选择。

Result: 在合成数据与真实数据集上进行大量实验：蒸馏SSM相对真实输出的近似误差低于0.7%；在下游文本分类与推理任务上，相比基线示例选择方法，FLOPs降低14.2倍，准确率提升6.48%。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过分层蒸馏得到的SSM能有效近似Transformer行为，并可用于高效的示例选择，在长上下文设定下同时实现显著的计算节省与性能提升。

Abstract: We study the problem of demonstration selection, which involves selecting a subset of examples for prepending to a query to a language model. This problem is closely related to in-context learning and language model inference. Since the inference cost of a transformer model scales quadratically with sequence length, the selection problem becomes especially challenging in a long-context scenario. In this paper, we tackle this problem by building on state space models (SSMs), which require only linear inference time given the input. Our approach involves two algorithms. The first learns a small set of SSMs through distillation of a (trained) transformer model. We partition all the layers into consecutive groups. Then for each group, we estimate a separate state space model to replicate the input-output behavior within the adjacent layers. Second, we map the distilled model outputs to a small set of tokens, and apply these embeddings for demonstration selection in downstream applications. We perform extensive experiments in both synthetic and real-world datasets to validate our approach. We demonstrate that the distilled SSMs only incur an approximation error of less than $0.7\%$ relative to the true output. In downstream evaluation, we show that on several text classification and reasoning tasks, our approach reduces FLOPs by $14.2\times$ and improves accuracy by $6.48\%$ relative to baseline demonstration selection methods.

</details>

*Benjamin Jäger,Nick Erickson,Léo Grinsztajn,Felix Birkel,Klemens Flöge,Oscar Key,Kürşat Kaya,Jonas Kübler,Adèle Frankel,Tobias Schröder,Anurag Garg,Jan Hendrik Metzen,David Salinas,Simon Bing,Kristina Collins,Tuana Çelik,Vahid Balazadeh,Lydia Sidhoum,Tomás Pereda,Brendan Roof,Andrej Tschalzev,Siyuan Guo,Philipp Singer,Lennart Purucker,Jake Robertson,Marie Salmon,Philipp Jund,Jerry Chen,Diana Kriuchkova,Arthur Cahu,Eliott Kalfon,Adrian Hayler,Georg Grab,Vitor Monteiro,Lilly Wehrhahn,Dominik Safaric,Clara Cornu,Alan Arazi,Rylee Grace,Simone Alessi,Mihir Manium,Bernhard Schölkopf,Yann LeCun,Madelon Hulsebos,Sauraj Gambhir,Noah Hollmann,Frank Hutter*
