Finally, a gap-origin study broke the fixed offset (both lr and batch move the gap ~2.3x; no external clock owns it) and revealed the law beneath: across 80 valid-anchor runs the anchor fires at 0.843 of time-to-emergence -- t_event ~= 1.19 x t_anchor -- and this multiplicative rule passed its own blind gate (5/5) at a third unseen configuration. False alarms are certified against 33 manufactured negatives. The precursor leads across 3 public model families (Pythia, OLMo, OLMo-2; 7 suites), with OLMo-2 at 1B tokens showing precursor formed, capability absent. Four pre-registered kill criteria fired and are reported. Every freeze precedes its data in a public commit chain.

</details>


### [99] [CompileRover: Revolutionizing Virtual Machine Compiler Optimization with a Tri-Role LLM-Driven Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19004)
*Mingqiao Mo,Yunlong Tan,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CompileRover——面向虚拟机编译器的优化框架，采用裁判、顾问、操作员三角色协作机制，结合控制流分析、代码结构变换与动态执行模式识别，在多个基准上超越现有最优虚拟机编译器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟机编译器现有输出常存在冗余计算、低效循环结构与非最优函数实现，导致执行效率受损，亟需针对性的优化框架。

Method: CompileRover 采用裁判（referee）、顾问（advisor）、操作员（operator）三角色协作机制，并综合运用全面优化算法与新颖方法，包括控制流分析、代码结构变换和动态执行模式识别，以突破性能瓶颈。

Result: 大量评估表明，CompileRover 持续优于当前最先进的虚拟机编译器，在多种基准测试中实现执行性能的显著提升；性能分析证实其优化显著降低执行开销、改善数据流一致性并稳健提升编译器性能。

Conclusion: CompileRover 是一种有效且可靠的虚拟机编译器优化方法，其三角色协作优化机制与配套算法可显著提升生成代码的执行效率与性能表现。

Abstract: Code optimization plays a crucial role in the development of virtual machine compilers, with optimization frameworks significantly enhancing the performance of generated assembly code. However, existing virtual machine compiler outputs frequently exhibit redundant computations, inefficient loop structures, and suboptimal function implementations, which collectively impair execution efficiency. To address these shortcomings, we propose CompileRover, an advanced optimization framework specifically designed for virtual machine compilers. CompileRover employs a sophisticated three-role collaboration mechanism, comprising a referee, an advisor, and an operator, effectively overcoming performance bottlenecks by leveraging comprehensive optimization algorithms and novel methodologies, including control flow analysis, code structure transformations, and dynamic execution pattern recognition. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that CompileRover consistently surpasses state-of-the-art virtual machine compilers, achieving significant improvements in execution performance across various benchmarks. Furthermore, performance analyses validate that the introduced optimizations notably reduce execution overhead, improve dataflow consistency, and robustly enhance compiler performance, showcasing CompileRover as an effective and reliable approach to optimizing virtual machine compilers.

</details>


### [100] [Tabular Deep Learning vs Classical Machine Learning for Urban Land Cover Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19010)
*Muntasir Tabasum,Tanpia Tasnim,Md. Ekramul Islam,Al Zadid Sultan Bin Habib*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文基于UCI ULC数据集，在统一可复现流程下对比经典机器学习模型与表格深度学习（TDL）模型在城市土地覆盖分类任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市土地覆盖分类对城市规划、环境监测和可持续发展至关重要；该数据集具有高维、异构特征与类别不平衡等遥感典型挑战，需要系统比较经典机器学习与表格深度学习方法。

Method: 使用UCI机器学习库的ULC数据集，含高分辨率航拍影像衍生的表格特征，覆盖道路、树木、草地、水体等九类。在统一可复现流程中，基准测试Logistic Regression、SVM、Random Forest、XGBoost、CatBoost等经典模型，以及TabNet、FT-Transformer、TabTransformer、TabSeq、1D CNN等TDL模型；针对类别不平衡，TDL采用加权交叉熵损失。

Result: 采用准确率、宏精确率、宏召回率、宏F1、AUC-ROC及混淆矩阵评估。结果表明树集成仍是强基线，但当非线性交互显著且不平衡处理有效时，TDL模型可匹配或超越其性能。

Conclusion: 树集成与TDL模型在城市土地覆盖制图中各具互补优势，TDL在有效处理类别不平衡与非线性交互时具备竞争力。

Abstract: Urban Land Cover (ULC) classification plays a crucial role in urban planning, environmental monitoring, and sustainable development. We study this task using the ULC dataset from the UCI Machine Learning Repository, which includes tabular features derived from high-resolution aerial imagery across nine classes (e.g., roads, trees, grass, water). The dataset presents typical remote sensing challenges, including high dimensionality, heterogeneous features, and class imbalance. In a unified, reproducible pipeline, we benchmark classical machine learning models (e.g., Logistic Regression, SVM, Random Forest, XGBoost, CatBoost) against Tabular Deep Learning (TDL) models (TabNet, FT-Transformer, TabTransformer, TabSeq, and 1D CNNs). To address class imbalance, we employ weighted cross-entropy loss for TDL models and evaluate performance using accuracy, macro-precision, macro-recall, macro-F1, AUC-ROC, and confusion matrices. Our results show that while tree ensembles remain strong general baselines, TDL models can match or exceed their performance when non-linear interactions are significant and imbalance handling is effective, providing complementary advantages for urban land cover mapping. See code: https://github.com/mtesha/tdl-vs-ml-urbanlandcover

</details>


### [101] [TwinMark: A Unified Watermark for Provable Survival Under Feature and Logit Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19011)
*Redwanul Karim,Tobias Feigl,Christopher Mutschler,Felix Ott*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 TwinMark 水印方案：用两个互补线性泛函（cov-Feat 协方差投影器与 cc-FALC 类条件 Fisher 对齐载体）读取同一 SHAKE128 密钥，覆盖分类器 API（KL-KD）与仅表征宿主（FM-KD）两类提取面，并给出教师可测的后验认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署的视觉模型存在两条水印提取面——分类器 API 与仅表征宿主，且分别遭受 KL 知识蒸馏与特征匹配 KD 攻击；单一读出口难以同时覆盖两面对抗、并给出可测的检测功效下界。

Method: 以单一位向量共享于两路读出：cov-Feat 对载体集协方差做投影；cc-FALC 由类均值 logits 解码类条件 Fisher 对齐线性载体。两通道经受限 OR 规则结合，检验统计量（校准零假设或比特投票）按暴露面选择；各自给出教师可测后验证书（cov-Feat 为秩盲算子范数证书，cc-FALC 为中心化 logit-gap 证书）。

Result: 在 K=1024、m=100 类（10.24× 过编码）时，比特投票达 z=23.0σ，教师精度代价仅 +0.9±0.2%p；CIFAR-10/100 与 Mini-ImageNet 上 13 种攻击中，凡攻击后仍保留任务效用的单元均通过验证；跨架构蒸馏（ResNet-18/50、VGG-16、MobileNet-V3）存活，并迁移至 GNSS 少样本、VOC 检测、ISIC 分割与 STL-10 SimCLR。

Conclusion: 双读出互补设计可同时覆盖分类器与仅表征两类提取面，在多种攻击与架构、以及跨任务域场景下保持可验证性与低精度代价。

Abstract: We propose TwinMark, a watermarking scheme that reads a single SHAKE128 secret through two complementary linear functionals of model-output summaries: a covariance projector against the carrier-set covariance (cov-Feat) and a class-conditional Fisher-aligned linear carrier decoded from class-mean logits (cc-FALC). The two readouts share one bit vector and cover the two extraction surfaces of a deployed vision model: a classifier API attacked by KL knowledge distillation (KD) (Std. KL-KD), and a representation-only host attacked by feature-matching KD (FM-KD). Each readout admits a teacher-measurable a posteriori certificate that lower-bounds post-distillation detection power, and the two channels combine under a regime-restricted OR rule whose test statistic (calibrated null or bit vote) is selected by the exposed surface. cov-Feat admits a rank-blind operator-norm certificate, cc-FALC admits a centered-logit-gap certificate that decouples bit capacity from class count: at K=1024 in m=100 classes (a 10.24x over-encoding), the bit-vote attains z=23.0 sigma at a teacher-accuracy cost of +0.9+-0.2%p. Across 13 attacks on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Mini-ImageNet, TwinMark verifies on every cell whose post-attack model retains task utility, survives cross-architecture distillation onto ResNet-18/50, VGG-16, and MobileNet-V3, and ports to GNSS few-shot, VOC detection, ISIC segmentation, and STL-10 SimCLR.

</details>


### [102] [Integrated Optimization of Automated Warehouse Operations and Last-Mile Transport for Differentiated On-Demand Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19048)
*Xiaozhu Sun,Bilal Farooq*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AGV智能仓储与末端多式联运的集成优化方法，用深度强化学习实现多目标联合调度：仓储端用MORM-AGDQN改进算法，运输端用MRMH-HCVRP框架，显著提升准时率并大幅缩短配送时间与距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向差异化按需配送场景，仓储作业与末端运输两系统相互独立，难以实现高吞吐连续订单调度、兼顾相互冲突的多目标需求，且整体系统灵敏度与适应性不足，亟需建立两系统间的动态联动机制。

Method: 设计多目标联合调度的深度强化学习算法，动态连接仓储与末端运输两系统；仓储优化采用基于多目标的Multi-Reward Machines-A* Guided Deep Q-Network（MORM-AGDQN）改进算法，综合服务水平、系统成本与外部运输需求；外部运输优化采用Multi-Reward, Multi-Head attention-Heterogeneous Capacity Vehicle Routing Problem（MRMH-HCVRP）框架，融入优化后的调度顺序与分组，并结合客户位置、需求、优先级及车辆容量、速度、服务范围。

Result: 该框架显著优于传统方法：仓储作业准时交付率达100%；联合优化后末端平均配送时间缩短29.3%–53.2%，总运输距离减少46.4%，高优先级服务率提升至92%以上，并在运营成本与客户满意度之间取得平衡。

Conclusion: 所提出的AGV仓储与末端多式联运集成优化框架，通过深度强化学习驱动的多目标联合调度，可有效协调仓储与配送两端，在提升服务时效与优先级满足度的同时降低运输成本，验证了该方法在按需配送场景下的有效性与适应性。

Abstract: In the context of differentiated on-demand goods delivery services, this study proposes an integrated optimization method for automated guided vehicles (AGVs) based smart warehouse operations and the last-mile multi-modal transport. A deep reinforcement learning algorithm for multi-objective joint scheduling is designed to establish a dynamic connection between two systems, solving key challenges such as achieving high-throughput continuous order scheduling, meeting competing requirements, and improving the overall system sensitivity and adaptability. For warehouse optimization within this framework, we propose an improved algorithm based on multi-objective, Multi-Reward Machines-A* Guided Deep Q-Network (MORM-AGDQN), which combines service level, system cost, and external transportation demand. For external optimization, we propose an improved algorithm based on a Multi-Reward, Multi Head attention-Heterogeneous Capacity Vehicle Routing Problem (MRMH-HCVRP) framework, which incorporates the optimized scheduling order sequence and grouping, combined with customer location, demand, and priority, vehicle capacity, speed, and service range. The results show that the proposed framework significantly outperforms traditional methods, achieving 100% on-time delivery rate for warehousing operations. After joint optimization, the average delivery time for the last mile was reduced by 29.3% to 53.2%, the total transportation distance was reduced by 46.4%, the high-priority service rate was increased to over 92%, and a balance was maintained between operating costs and customer satisfaction.

</details>


### [103] [LightSleepX: A Lightweight, Inception-Based Dual-Modal Network for Sleep Staging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19062)
*Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出轻量级睡眠分期框架LightSleepX，结合Inception式深度可分离卷积与多尺度增强注意力进行EEG/EOG多模态特征提取，并采用Mamba编码器做长程时序建模，在Sleep-EDF-20和ISRUC-S3上分别取得85.9%/0.803和81.8%/0.796的准确率/宏F1，仅0.049M参数与195.9 MFLOPs，面向资源受限场景的本地部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动睡眠分期是个人健康监测的基础，但现有方法难以落地：传统流程依赖手工特征或浅层模型，泛化能力不足；先进深度学习方法虽准确，却计算开销大，不适合资源受限环境。同时，实际本地部署对计算成本与隐私保护有硬性约束。

Method: LightSleepX采用双模块设计：（1）特征提取端采用Inception风格架构，结合深度可分离卷积与多尺度增强注意力（Multi-scale Enhanced Attention），实现EEG/EOG多模态高效特征提取；（2）时序建模端采用Mamba编码器，实现无需规则（rule-free）的长程时序依赖建模。

Result: 在公开基准数据集上，Sleep-EDF-20上达到85.9%准确率与0.803宏F1；跨被试ISRUC-S3上达到81.8%准确率与0.796宏F1；模型规模为0.049M参数、195.9 MFLOPs。

Conclusion: LightSleepX以极低计算开销实现与重型模型相当的睡眠分期性能，并具备跨被试泛化能力，适用于计算成本与隐私为核心约束的本地化部署场景，为资源受限环境下的实用睡眠分析提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Automatic sleep staging is fundamental to personal health monitoring, yet many existing approaches are ill-suited for real-world applications. Traditional pipelines often rely on hand-crafted features or shallow machine learning models that struggle to generalize, while state-of-the-art deep learning methods, though accurate, are computationally heavy and impractical for resource-constrained environments. This paper introduces LightSleepX, a lightweight framework designed to deliver robust sleep analysis in resource-constrained environments. LightSleepX combines an Inception-style architecture with depthwise separable convolutions and Multi-scale Enhanced Attention for efficient multi-modal EEG/EOG feature extraction, and a Mamba encoder for rule-free long-range temporal modeling. On public benchmark datasets, LightSleepX achieves 85.9% accuracy and a 0.803 macro-F1 score on Sleep-EDF-20, and 81.8% accuracy and a 0.796 macro-F1 score on the cross-subject ISRUC-S3 dataset. With 0.049M parameters and 195.9 MFLOPs, the framework targets practical local deployment where computational cost and privacy are central constraints.

</details>


### [104] [RLLBC-Lib: An Educational Code Library for Reinforcement Learning and Learning-Based Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19074)
*Bernd Frauenknecht,Emma Cramer,Artur Eisele,Paul Kruse,Lukas Kesper,Jonas Hertrampf,Ramil Sabirov,Jyotirmaya Patra,Johannes Berger,Paul Brunzema,Friedrich Solowjow,Sebastian Trimpe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文介绍了RLLBC-Lib，一个面向学习型控制场景的强化学习教学代码库，通过表格型RL与深度RL两套库传达统一设计原则，并支持自动评分编程作业。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习因多对象、多周期的复杂交互而难以入门，现有实现往往不够直观，亟需一个易于上手的教学工具以降低学生和学习者的进入门槛。

Method: 设计并实现RLLBC-Lib代码库：核心是完整的表格型RL算法库以夯实理论基础；深度RL库遵循相同的设计原则，凸显二者之间的对应关系；并提供展示RL核心原理、对比RL与其他学习型控制方法的实现集合。

Result: 该库提供了表格型RL、深度RL以及原理演示与对比实现的完整集合，并可作为自动评分编程作业的基础平台。

Conclusion: RLLBC-Lib通过统一设计的表格与深度RL实现降低了RL学习门槛，兼顾理论理解与实践教学，适用于课程作业与自动评分布置。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is an exciting concept as well as a remarkable success story worth sharing. However, RL builds on rather complex interactions between different objects that play out over several cycles. Such dynamics are often best explained with an easily accessible implementation. We present RLLBC-Lib, a carefully crafted code library with the goal of lowering the entry barrier for students and other learners of RL in the context of learning-based control. At its heart, RLLBC-Lib comprises a comprehensive library of tabular RL approaches to enforce a clear understanding of the theoretical foundations. A deep RL library follows the same design principles, underscoring the parallels between simple tabular and state-of-the-art deep RL approaches. Additionally, RLLBC-Lib provides a collection of implementations illustrating core RL principles and contrasting RL to other learning-based control approaches. Finally, RLLBC-Lib provides an ideal basis for creating programming assignments with automated grading.

</details>


### [105] [Double descent is the principle of least action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19076)
*Congzhou M Sha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文用统计力学解释神经网络中的双下降（double descent）现象：将基于随机梯度的训练轨迹视为在训练损失能量景观上、处于温度 T 的粒子随机游走，借助玻尔兹曼分布与能量均分定理说明为何测试误差随参数量 d 先降、后升、再降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试误差随参数量 d 的变化呈现"下降—在模型刚好能拟合训练数据时达到峰值—再次下降"的双下降现象，此前缺乏统一的物理解释。作者希望用统计力学框架给出机制性解释。

Method: 把随机梯度训练轨迹建模为在训练损失能量景观上以诱导温度 T 随机游走的粒子；假设已平衡的运行按玻尔兹曼分布等概率访问相同训练损失的每个参数向量（统计力学基本假设）。由于训练从初始点出发、扩散时间有限，等效引入权重衰减，使每个参数成为二次自由度；再用能量均分定理将能量按 T/2 分摊到 d 个自由度。

Result: 在固定训练损失下，增加参数量会降低温度，使玻尔兹曼分布向平稳路径（stationary path）集中；同时增加参数量只会降低平稳路径的 L2 范数，因此在固定损失下采样到的解随 d 增大而更不易变大，等效增强了权重正则化。

Conclusion: 双下降现象可由统计力学得到解释：参数量增加通过降温与平稳路径范数下降两种机制，共同起到隐式正则化作用，从而在过参数化区域使测试误差重新下降。

Abstract: The test error of a model plotted against its number of parameters $d$ falls, peaks when the model can just fit the training data, and falls again, exhibiting the double descent phenomenon. We explain the phenomenon with statistical mechanics. The training trajectory of a stochastic gradient-based method is a particle wandering over the energy landscape of the training loss at an induced temperature $T$, and a run that has equilibrated visits every parameter vector of a given training loss equally often, the fundamental postulate of statistical mechanics, with probability given by the Boltzmann distribution. Because training starts at an initial point and has only finite time to diffuse, it carries an effective weight decay, which makes every parameter a quadratic degree of freedom. The equipartition theorem then distributes the energy among the $d$ degrees of freedom in shares of $T/2$, so at a fixed training loss adding parameters lowers the temperature and drives the Boltzmann distribution toward the stationary path. Finally, adding parameters can only lower the $L^2$ norm of the stationary path, so a solution sampled at fixed loss is less likely to be large with increasing $d$, effectively increasing weight regularization.

</details>


### [106] [Probabilistic Linear Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19077)
*Frederic Koriche,Jean-Marie Lagniez,Chi Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于稀疏锚定线性模型的统一概率可解释性框架，适用于二分类和连续回归，严格推广了基于子集的解释方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化可解释性虽能提供数学依据，但溯因解释常涉及过多特征、超出人类认知极限，而概率松弛方法大多局限于分类任务。作者希望构建一个统一框架，兼顾稀疏性、锚定性与回归/分类的通用性。

Method: 将实例映射到布尔超立方体，构造捕获特征贡献幅度与方向的稀疏（预算k）锚定线性解释。证明在有神经网络基础下最小化相关性误差是 ClassNPPP-hard，并将其与可处理的保真度误差关联：对参数化局部分布族，任意k-稀疏解释的相关性误差被保真度误差以局部较小的乘性因子界定。采用两种方法求解：混合整数规划（MIP）给出可证明最优解并保持多项式样本复杂度，以及多项式时间的迭代硬阈值（IHT）算法并具近似保证。

Result: 实验表明，与 LIME、MAPLE 等先进基线相比，本方法构造的解释同时满足锚定与稀疏约束，并始终实现更低的相关性误差。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了分类与回归的概率可解释性，将难解的相关性误差目标与可处理的保真度误差联系起来，并通过MIP与IHT两种互补方法实现高效求解，在理论与实验上均优于现有基线。

Abstract: Formal explainability provides mathematically grounded justifications for individual predictions. However, abductive explanations often exceed human cognitive limits by involving too many features, while probabilistic relaxations have remained largely limited to categorical classification. We present a unified framework for probabilistic explainability based on sparse, anchored linear models, applicable to both binary classification and continuous regression. By mapping instances to the Boolean hypercube, our linear explanations strictly generalize subset-based approaches: they capture both the magnitude and direction of feature contributions while enforcing a prescribed sparsity budget $k$. We show that minimizing the relevance error for such explanations is \ClassNPPP-hard when the underlying model is a neural network, and we relate this intractable objective to a tractable surrogate---the fidelity error. For a parameterized family of local distributions, the relevance error of any $k$-sparse explanation is bounded by its fidelity error up to a multiplicative factor that remains small locally. We address the resulting empirical problem using two complementary approaches: a Mixed Integer Programming (MIP) formulation that yields provably optimal empirical solutions while maintaining polynomial sample complexity, and a polynomial-time Iterative Hard Thresholding (IHT) algorithm with provable approximation guarantees. Empirical evaluations show that, unlike state-of-the-art baselines such as LIME and MAPLE, our explanations satisfy both the anchoring and sparsity constraints by construction, while consistently achieving lower relevance error.

</details>


### [107] [Evidence-Grounded Agentic Formulation Development in an Autonomous Laboratory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19099)
*Michael M. Craig,Riley J. Hickman,Yingshan Ma,Rémi Piché-Taillefer,Christine Allen,Pauric Bannigan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Andromeda 2 是一个可推理内部结构化实验证据并调用计算/实验工具的智能体系统，用于自乳化给药系统（SEDDS）配方开发；在与 Andromeda 1 及湿实验 DoE 的对比中，显著提升高性能命中率与 TPP 达标数，并实现更高的紫杉醇载药量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SEDDS 虽可提升难溶性药物的口服生物利用度，但筛选高性能配方仍高度依赖密集实验，成本高、效率低，因此需要更高效的配方设计与优化方法。

Method: 提出 Andromeda 2 智能体系统：基于结构化内部实验证据进行推理，并调用计算与实验工具，连续设计并执行多批次配方；在匹配预算下使用小型化自动化实验室，与已部署于多个实际开发项目的概率优化模型 Andromeda 1 及湿实验 DoE 方案进行基准对比；并进行消融实验评估结构化内部实验证据的作用。

Result: 针对紫杉醇，Andromeda 2 的高性能命中率为 50%，Andromeda 1 为 17%，DoE 为 2%；满足全部四项 TPP 目标的配方数分别为 12、6、0。AUC10-240 中位数分别为 70.1、12.0、3.5 mg·min/mL；Andromeda 2 与 Andromeda 1 的最大 AUC 相当。所选全 TPP 配方在首次 FaSSIF 测定中实现表观有效紫杉醇载药量 19 ± 5% w/w，约为已发表紫杉醇 S-SEDDS 的 5.7% w/w 的 3.3 倍。消融实验显示，获取结构化内部实验证据使平均 AUC 提高 34%。

Conclusion: Andromeda 2 能在匹配预算下显著优于概率优化模型和传统 DoE，提高 SEDDS 配方开发效率与质量；结构化内部实验证据对性能提升具有关键贡献。

Abstract: Self-emulsifying drug delivery systems (SEDDS) can improve the oral bioavailability of poorly soluble drugs, but identifying high-performing formulations remains experimentally intensive. We present Andromeda 2, an agentic system that reasons over structured in-house experimental evidence and invokes computational and experimental tools to design and execute successive formulation batches. Using a miniaturized automated laboratory at a matched budget, we benchmark it against Andromeda 1, a probabilistic optimization model deployed across dozens of live development projects, and a wet-lab design-of-experiments (DoE) campaign. For paclitaxel, Andromeda 2 achieved a 50% high-performance hit rate versus 17% for Andromeda 1 and 2% for DoE, and identified 12 formulations meeting all four target product profile (TPP) objectives versus 6 and 0, respectively. Median $AUC_{10-240}$ was 70.1, 12.0, and 3.5 mg$\cdot$min/mL, while maximum AUC was comparable between Andromeda 2 and Andromeda 1. A selected full-TPP formulation achieved an apparent effective paclitaxel loading of $19 \pm 5\%$ w/w at the first FaSSIF measurement, approximately 3.3-fold higher than the 5.7% w/w loading reported for a published paclitaxel S-SEDDS. A controlled ablation showed that access to structured in-house experimental evidence increased mean AUC by 34%.

</details>


### [108] [How Model Growth, Recursion, and Boundary Operators Influence Scaling Exponents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19107)
*Zixi Chen,Akshay Vegesna,Samip Dahal,Andrew Gordon Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 架构干预（循环/递归深度、边界算子）能改变预训练scaling law的指数，带来随算力增长而增大的效率收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为scaling law的指数由数据与任务决定，架构选择只能改变常数项而难以改变指数。作者希望探究架构干预是否能够修改scaling exponent，从而在算力增加时获得指数级的性能提升。

Method: 以looped transformer（递归深度）作为锚点，通过训练中增加循环次数实现模型增长（含共享权重与非共享权重两种形式）；并在vanilla transformer中引入boundary operator，对早期block进行归一化并注入；同时在数据受限、多epoch的设置下考察标准looping的正则化效果。

Result: 带/不带共享权重的模型增长对scaling exponent影响最大：7.4B的模型增长架构在CORE上以约20倍更少算力匹敌GPT-3 13B，且算力效率收益随规模增大而提升。boundary operator也带来效率收益，但幅度较小。在数据受限多epoch场景中，标准looping具有正则化作用，且随规模增大最优循环次数应增加。

Conclusion: 结果可从computational depth视角统一解释：在给定算力预算下，提升transformer的可用深度可带来随规模增长而放大的效率收益。

Abstract: Scaling laws predict how loss decreases with increases in computation. We show, contrary to conventional wisdom, that architectural interventions can modify scaling exponents in pre-training, leading to exponential improvements in performance with increases in computation. As an anchoring point, we consider the architectural formulation of looped transformers. Although not typically used in this way, looping, also known as recursive depth, provides a mechanism for model growth, by increasing the number of loops during training. Model growth, with and without shared weights, provides the biggest changes to the scaling exponents. In particular, a 7.4B model growth architecture matches GPT-3 13B on CORE with roughly $20\times$ less compute, and has compute efficiency gains that increase with scale. Moreover, simply using a boundary operator in a vanilla transformer, which normalizes and injects an earlier block, also provides increasing compute-efficiency gains, although to a lesser extent. In the data-constrained, multi-epoch setting, standard looping has a useful regularizing effect, where we find it is compute-optimal to increase the number of loops with scale. These results can be understood through the lens of computational depth: for a given computational budget, we wish to increase the usable depth of the transformer, which can lead to efficiency gains that increase with scale.

</details>


### [109] [Exponential Hardness of Off-Policy Evaluation under History-Dependent Logging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19135)
*Pranaya Jajoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构造了反例：即使日志数据覆盖所有隐藏状态、满足动作覆盖、信念覆盖与行为边际结果揭示条件，历史依赖的记录器仍可使目标策略价值评估样本复杂度达 Θ((3/2)^H)，呈指数级不可学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答 Zhang & Jiang (2025) 提出的历史依赖记录、基于模型的自评估可学习性问题：数据"覆盖充分"是否就意味着信息充分？作者要检验行为边际揭示定义下能否保证可处理性。

Method: 对每个 H≥3 构造两个 POMDP（每阶段≤2 隐状态、3 动作），共用含 3 个记忆状态的历史依赖记录器；使动作覆盖、信念覆盖及两个行为边际结果揭示条件均与 H 无关。核心机制是"重置"擦除决定目标价值的未知转移；精确刻画所得统计实验并给出匹配的最优估计器，并用有向双车道 gridworld 实现与轨迹仿真验证。

Result: 在置信度 1-δ（0<δ≤1/4）下，即使两个候选模型已知，将确定性目标策略评估到精度 1/8 仍需 Θ((3/2)^H log(1/δ)) 条日志回合，指数下界；有限样本仿真与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 历史依赖记录 + 行为边际揭示定义下，基于模型的目标策略评估不可处理；说明此类覆盖式揭示条件不足以保证可学习性，为自评估设定划定了内在困难边界。

Abstract: Can a logged dataset visit every hidden state frequently and still be exponentially uninformative about a target policy's value? We show that it can when the logger depends on history. For every horizon $H \ge 3$, we construct two POMDPs with at most two latent states per stage, three actions, and a common logger with three memory states. Action coverage, belief coverage, and two behavior-marginal outcome-revealing conditions all have constants independent of $H$. Nevertheless, evaluating a known deterministic target policy to accuracy $1/8$ requires $Θ((3/2)^H \log(1/δ))$ logged episodes at confidence $1-δ$, for $0 < δ\le 1/4$, even when both candidate models are known. The mechanism is simple: a reset erases the unknown transition that determines the target value. We characterize the resulting statistical experiment exactly and obtain a matching optimal estimator. A directed two-lane gridworld realizes the construction, and trajectory simulations agree with its finite-sample prediction. The result establishes intractability for the history-dependent-logging, model-based case posed by Zhang and Jiang (2025, arXiv:2503.01134), under their behavior-marginal definition of revealing.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [Not All Agents Are Equal: Code Quality and Post-Merge Maintenance Across Five Autonomous Coding Agents in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17598)
*Obada Kraishan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文基于AIDev数据集与58,792条GitHub API缓存响应，分析了2024年12月至2025年7月间2,807个仓库中来自五家商业编码代理的37,623个带来源标注的PR，考察其代码质量、安全性、维护性与人工审查行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主编码代理已能大规模在公共仓库提交PR，但学界对其代码合并后的质量、安全性与维护性后果了解甚少，因此需要系统性的实证研究。

Method: 将AIDev数据集与58,792条缓存GitHub API响应结合，针对五家商业代理（OpenAI Codex、Devin、GitHub Copilot、Cursor、Claude Code）及匹配的人类基线，测量新增代码中的安全异味、结构可维护性、合并后churn、回滚率与人工审查行为。

Result: 质量差异因供应商而异：Codex PR回滚率约为人类的一半（6.1% vs 11.5%，OR=0.50），Devin回滚率更高（14.5%，OR=1.31）；代理代码整体比人类代码更少出现安全异味（OR=0.63），主要源于硬编码凭证与eval类构造更少；审查投入不均：Copilot PR获得最多人工审查与变更请求，Claude Code PR首次人工审查中位等待最长（12.6小时）。

Conclusion: 代理生成代码的质量与风险高度依赖具体供应商，不应一概而论；代理代码在安全异味上整体优于人类代码，但审查负担分布不均，相关流水线代码、统计报告与图表已公开以供复现。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents now open pull requests in public repositories at a scale that was out of reach two years ago, yet little is known about what happens to that code after it lands. This paper studies 37,623 provenance-labeled pull requests (PRs) from five commercial agents (OpenAI Codex, Devin, GitHub Copilot, Cursor, and Claude Code) and a matched human baseline, drawn from 2,807 GitHub repositories between December 2024 and July 2025. We combine the AIDev dataset with 58,792 cached GitHub API responses to measure security smells in added code, structural maintainability, post-merge churn, revert rates, and human review behavior. Three results stand out. First, quality differences are vendor-specific rather than uniform: Codex-authored PRs were reverted about half as often as human PRs (6.1% vs. 11.5%, odds ratio 0.50), while Devin PRs were reverted more often (14.5%, odds ratio 1.31). Second, agent code pooled across vendors was less likely than human code to contain a security smell (odds ratio 0.63), driven by fewer hardcoded credentials and eval-style constructs. Third, review effort concentrates unevenly: Copilot PRs drew the most human reviews and change requests, and Claude Code PRs waited the longest for a first human review (median 12.6 hours). All pipeline code, statistical reports, and figures are released for replication.

</details>


### [111] [Quiescence in Concert: Composing Multi-Channel Time-Outs for IOCO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17640)
*Laura Brandán Briones,Petra van den Bos,Marcus Gerhold*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一种多通道提升（multi-channel lifting）算子，将带超时的实时测试规范从普通标记转移系统自动提升为时间自动机，并证明其与并行组合可交换且保持MBT的符合关系、测试生成与判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时系统的测试理论虽丰富，但因时间自动机等机制需要专业知识，实践中应用不足。作者此前的"经典提升算子"让建模者只需用普通标记转移系统描述行为，即可隐式获得表达其静默行为的定时自动机；但该工作未处理每个组件在各自通道上各自拥有超时的情形，本文旨在将提升推广到多通道带超时的场景。

Method: 引入多通道提升算子：每个组件携带其自身通道上的独立time-out，据此把普通标记转移系统隐式映射为表达静默行为的时间自动机；并形式化证明该提升与并行组合算子的可交换性（compose后lift 等价于 lift后compose）。

Result: 证明了多通道提升与并行组合可交换；证明MBT框架在提升下保持：在基于超时的测试器可观察的可测轨迹上，一致性关系（conformance）、测试生成与测试判定（verdicts）均被提升所保持。

Conclusion: 多通道提升使实时测试在保留既有MBT理论工具的同时，免除了使用者掌握定时机制专业知识的需求，提升了实时MBT方法的实用性与可组合性。

Abstract: In Model-Based Testing (MBT), test suites are generated automatically from a formal specification. The theory of testing real-time systems is rich, but often underused in practice, partly because applying the timed machinery demands expertise practitioners should not need. In prior work we addressed this for timed testing with a canonic lifting operator, which lets a modeller specify behaviour as plain labelled transition systems while implicitly obtaining the timed automata that express their quiescent behaviour (the explicit absence of outputs) with timers. This paper takes the next step: we show that our lifting still works when each component carries its own time-out on its own channel. This way, we introduce a multi-channel lifting. We show that it commutes with parallel composition, i.e. composing and lifting is the same as lifting first and then composing. We show that the MBT apparatus survives: conformance, test generation and verdicts are preserved by the lifting, on the testable traces that a time-out based tester can observe.

</details>


### [112] [CARE: A Responsibility-Oriented Architecture for Domain-Scoped Resolution in Modular and Upgradeable Smart Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17647)
*Ali Rajabi Nekoo,Laleh Rasoul,Azadeh Zamanifar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 CARE——一种面向职责的智能合约架构，通过域范围能力解析显式划分协调、解析、共享状态、可复用服务与观测的归属边界；以形式化模型导出五项结构属性，并用 TLA+ 与 TLC 验证七项架构不变量。受控实验显示相对 EIP-2535 与纯路由基线引入约 7.7k gas 的固定路由开销，而局部能力替换成本接近纯路由基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化可升级合约系统虽支持可替换执行逻辑，但仅靠模块化分发无法界定各组件的架构职责归属（协调、解析、共享状态、可复用服务、观测），且执行协调与"选择执行目标的权限"未被分离。

Method: 提出 CARE 职责导向架构：显式化职责所有权边界，引入域范围能力解析，分离执行协调与执行目标选择权限；建立形式化架构模型并导出五项结构属性；用 TLA+ 编码行为语义，以 TLC 对七项架构不变量进行评估，并用中间 TLC 结果迭代精化模型后执行最终验证；针对 EIP-2535 与纯路由基线开展受控实证评测，并辅以对抗性测试。

Result: 在受测负载中，CARE 引入约 7.7k gas 的近似固定运行时路由溢价；局部能力替换成本接近纯路由对照组，且显著低于所评测的 EIP-2535 重配置路径。对抗性测试进一步刻画了授权控制及委托执行与重入相关的残余风险。

Conclusion: CARE 定位为一种实现显式职责归属与局部化演进的架构模型，而非对现有智能合约可升级机制的通用替代方案。

Abstract: Modular and upgradeable smart-contract systems enable replaceable execution logic, but modular dispatch alone does not define which components own architectural responsibilities such as coordination, resolution, shared state, reusable services, and observation. This paper presents CARE, a responsibility-oriented architecture that makes these ownership boundaries explicit and introduces domain-scoped capability resolution, separating execution coordination from the authority that selects an execution target. CARE is defined through a formal architectural model from which five structural properties are derived. Its behavioral semantics are further encoded in TLA+ and evaluated with TLC against seven architectural invariants. Intermediate TLC results were used to refine the architectural model before the final validation run. A controlled empirical evaluation against EIP-2535 and a routing only baseline shows that CARE introduces an approximately fixed 7.7k-gas runtime routing premium in the evaluated workloads, while localized capability replacement remains close to the routing-only control and substantially less expensive than the evaluated EIP-2535 reconfiguration path. Adversarial tests further characterize authorization controls and residual risks associated with delegated execution and reentrancy. These results position CARE as an architectural model for explicit responsibility ownership and localized evolution rather than as a universal replacement for existing smart-contract upgradeability mechanisms

</details>


### [113] [A Large-Scale Empirical Study of Quality Assurance Practices and Gaps in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17698)
*Wuyang Dai,Moses Openja,Jiho Shin,Hung Viet Pham,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文对157个开源LLM智能体项目进行大规模实证研究，刻画其质量保障（QA）实践中的执行面、防护措施、测试产物与风险缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体整合规划、记忆、工具调用、代码执行与外部交互，自主性提升的同时引入可靠性、安全性与安全性新风险，但现有QA实践是否足以应对尚不清楚。

Method: 选取GitHub星标≥100的157个开源LLM智能体项目，分析其文档、源代码、配置与测试，从执行面、防护措施、测试产物、风险场景与反复出现的缺口五个维度刻画QA实践。

Result: 当前QA主要聚焦基础功能与高风险动作，覆盖呈碎片化；等效执行路径间防护措施应用不一致；测试很少考察边界、对抗或多步工具调用失败；已识别风险鲜少转化为端到端QA检查。

Conclusion: 需从功能级测试转向系统化端到端验证，确保智能体工作流在与不可信输入、工具、持久状态及外部API交互时仍处于预期边界内。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents are increasingly used across software engineering, web automation, research, and productivity applications. Their integration of planning, memory, tool use, code execution, and external interactions enables greater autonomy but also introduces new reliability, safety, and security risks. We present a large-scale empirical study of quality assurance (QA) practices in 157 open-source LLM-based agent projects with at least 100 GitHub stars. We analyze documentation, source code, configurations, and tests to characterize QA practices across execution surfaces, safeguards, testing artifacts, risk scenarios, and recurring gaps. We find that current QA primarily focuses on basic functionality and high-risk actions, while coverage remains fragmented. Safeguards are inconsistently applied across equivalent execution routes, tests rarely examine boundary, adversarial, or multi-step tool-use failures, and identified risks are seldom translated into end-to-end QA checks. These findings highlight the need to move beyond feature-level testing toward systematic end-to-end validation that ensures agent workflows remain within intended boundaries when interacting with untrusted inputs, tools, persistent state, and external APIs.

</details>


### [114] [Detecting Argument-Swap Bugs Using Context-Enhanced Code Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17844)
*Subrata Das,Ali Aman,Muhammad Asaduzzaman,Kawser Wazed Nafi,Salimur Choudhury*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出BugProbe，一种无需调用-定义映射的基于学习的Python参数顺序错误检测方法，融合多源上下文与名称相似性，构建大规模数据集与真实基准，检测效果优于SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 名称蕴含丰富语义信息，已被广泛用于缺陷检测等任务。已有方法通过方法实参与形参名的词法相似性检测参数顺序错误，但依赖调用与定义的映射关系，而在Python等动态类型语言中该映射难以获取，故需一种不依赖该映射的检测方法。

Method: BugProbe不依赖调用-定义映射，利用多源上下文信息（局部上下文与实参使用上下文），将基于名称的相似性与机器学习相结合，构建方法实参的表达性表示；数据方面从top-1000星标GitHub仓库收集132,739个Python源文件，生成3,371,244个合成训练样例，并人工核验提交历史构建含55个真实参数交换缺陷的基准。

Result: 在该数据集上评估，方法取得较高准确率，并在标准评价指标上持续优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 结果表明，无需显式调用-定义解析即可有效检测参数顺序缺陷，方法适用于动态类型语言场景。

Abstract: Names of source code elements convey rich semantic information and have been widely used in software engineering tasks such as bug detection, code completion, type prediction, and code classification. Prior studies exploit lexical similarity between method arguments and formal parameter names to detect bugs caused by incorrectly ordered arguments, typically relying on establishing mappings between method calls and their corresponding definitions. However, such mappings are often difficult to obtain in dynamically typed languages like Python. In this paper, we present BugProbe, a learning-based approach for detecting incorrectly ordered arguments in Python method calls that does not require call-to-definition mappings. Our approach leverages multiple sources of contextual information, including local context and argument usage context, and combines name-based similarity with machine learning to construct expressive representations of method arguments. We collect a new dataset of 132,739 Python source files from the top-1,000 starred GitHub repositories, yielding 3,371,244 synthetic training examples, and contribute a curated benchmark of 55 real-world argument-swap bugs manually verified from commit histories. We evaluate our approach on this dataset and show that it achieves high accuracy and consistently outperforms a state-of-the-art baseline across standard evaluation metrics. These results demonstrate that effective detection of argument-ordering bugs is possible without relying on explicit call-to-definition resolution, making the approach well suited for dynamically typed language settings.

</details>


### [115] [Experimental Settings in LLM-Based Program Repair: A Study of Inputs, Tool Access, Feedback, and Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17993)
*Xushu Dai,Yicheng Cai,Nanqing Luo,Pei-Yu Tseng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文指出仅报告基准、修复缺陷数与验证测试已不足以刻画自动程序修复任务，提出显式描述实验设置的框架与机器可读模式，以提高可复现性并明确跨系统比较的范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期基于LLM的APR系统在修复前提供的信息、修复中允许的仓库与测试操作、失败后返回的反馈方面存在差异，使得同一基准可实例化为从局部补丁生成到仓库级诊断与迭代修复等显著不同的修复任务；因此，仅凭基准身份无法重建被评估任务或确定修复率比较的适当范围。

Method: 提出一个显式刻画APR结果相关实验设置的框架，并据此分析Defects4J与SWE-bench上系统的实验设置，按任务单元、缺陷定位假设、初始输入、工具访问、修复时反馈、最终验证与资源预算等维度对每个结果进行刻画；同时引入机器可读的模式来规定每项实验设置。

Result: 分析表明基准身份本身不足以重建被评估的任务，也不足以确定跨已报告修复率比较的适当范围；机器可读模式可用于明确记录各实验设置。

Conclusion: 应显式、机器可读地规定实验设置，以提升可复现性并使跨系统比较的适用范围清晰可辨。

Abstract: Evaluations of automated program repair (APR) systems commonly report the benchmark, the number of repaired defects, and the tests used for final patch validation, but these items no longer fully specify the repair task presented to a system. Recent LLM-based systems differ in the information supplied before repair, the repository and testing operations permitted during repair, and the feedback returned after unsuccessful attempts, allowing the same benchmark to instantiate substantially different repair tasks ranging from localized patch generation to repository-level diagnosis and iterative repair. We present a framework for explicitly specifying the experimental settings associated with reported APR results. We analyze reported experimental settings from systems evaluated on Defects4J and SWE-bench and characterize each result by its task unit, fault-localization assumptions, initial input, tool access, repair-time feedback, final validation, and resource budget. Our analysis shows that benchmark identity alone is insufficient to reconstruct the evaluated task or determine the appropriate scope of comparison across reported repair rates. We therefore introduce a machine-readable schema for specifying each experimental setting to improve reproducibility and make the scope of cross-system comparisons explicit.

</details>


### [116] [An Empirical Evaluation of Cost-Efficient Large Language Models on Algorithmic Programming Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18052)
*Chandimal Adikari,Nandika Herath*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究实证评估低成本LLM生成企业级代码的可靠性：8种配置共生成7,593个符合签名的Spring Boot方法，结构合规接近满分，但仅12.9%返回正确答案，38.4%方法并未真正计算返回值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业希望用成本高效的LLM按书面规范生成生产代码，但其可信度缺乏实证依据，尤其在不允许迭代、禁止硬编码的严格约束下，模型是否会以"看似合规"的方式规避真实计算尚不清楚。

Method: 以992道算法题为素材，要求三种模型（Gemini Flash 3、GPT-5.4 mini、Claude Haiku 4.5）输出符合强制签名与DTO规范的Java Spring Boot服务方法；模型×智能体工具组合×两种提示词共形成八种配置，禁止迭代与硬编码，并保留8道缺输入题探测异常响应。对7,593个方法按八类结果分类法归类，随后部署执行，获得7,936次实测请求并与分类结果关联。

Result: 结构合规率接近上限；38.4%的方法未计算其返回值，仅12.9%的返回答案正确。按结果类别分层后，响应可靠性与正确性呈反向关系；真正执行计算的方法反而最少给出响应，其正确率为19.3%。

Conclusion: 低成本LLM在受约束企业代码生成中"形式合规"与"实质正确"严重脱节，仅凭编译通过与签名匹配无法判断可信度；需引入面向计算真实性的评测与运行期验证机制。

Abstract: This study empirically evaluates whether cost-efficient Large Language Models (LLMs) can be trusted to generate enterprise code to a written specification. Three models (Gemini Flash 3, GPT-5.4 mini and Claude Haiku 4.5) were asked to solve 992 algorithmic problems as Java Spring Boot service methods conforming to a mandated signature and data-transfer-object specification, crossing four model and agentic coding tool combinations with two prompt variants to yield eight configurations, with iteration forbidden and hardcoded answers explicitly prohibited. Eight problem statements were withheld to probe how models respond to missing input. The 7,593 resulting methods were classified by an eight-class outcome taxonomy describing what each does about producing an answer, then deployed and executed, giving 7,936 measured requests joined to that classification. Structural conformance approached ceiling, yet 38.4% of methods do not compute the value they returned and only 12.9% of returned answers were correct. Conditioning on outcome class shows that response reliability and correctness are inversely related, whereas genuinely computing methods answered least often and were correct 19.3%. Limitations include single generation runs per configuration, partial harness coverage, single-pass timing, syntactic classification, and probable corpus contamination.

</details>


### [117] [Relationally Guided Use Case Modeling with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18291)
*Guangyu Wang,Bangqi Li,Ji Wu,Zhijun Shao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出FlowGen方法，利用LLM语义信息处理、语义关系图和增强R-GAT，实现完整用例流程构建（基本流、分支点预测、备选流生成），在20个数据集上全面优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用例流程支撑需求分析、架构与详细设计、测试用例生成等下游软件工程活动，但人工构建成本高且依赖专家经验；现有自动化方法难以保持语义一致性、控制流逻辑、数据流逻辑及系统边界，尤其在识别分支点和生成备选流方面表现不足。

Method: FlowGen包含四个环节：基于LLM的语义信息处理（SIP）提取语义元素；构建由增强R-GAT编码的语义关系图（SRG）用于基本流生成（BFGen）；通过BPP进行分支点预测；通过AFGen生成分支条件下的备选流。

Result: 在13个公开数据集和7个工业数据集上，三个核心组件均持续优于竞争基线。BFGen的Precision提升14%，Recall提升7-25%，F1提升11-30%，AUC提升10-19%；BPP的Precision提升30-110%，Recall提升33-91%，F1提升32-117%；AFGen的Precision提升8-23%，F1提升5-18%，AUC提升0.6-2.5%。

Conclusion: 验证了LLM-based SIP模块与BFGen中注意力保留因子的有效性，分析了需求完整性对BFGen的影响，并考察了不同分支相关上下文范围对AFGen的影响。

Abstract: Use case flows are important elements of use case modeling because they support downstream software engineering activities, including requirements analysis, architectural and detailed design, and test case generation. However, constructing them manually is costly and expertise-intensive, while existing automated approaches still struggle to preserve semantic consistency, control-flow logic, data-flow logic, and the intended system boundary, especially when identifying branch points and generating alternative flows. To address this problem, we propose FlowGen for complete use case flow construction. FlowGen uses LLM-based Semantic Information Processing (SIP) to extract semantic elements, constructs a Semantic Relational Graph (SRG) encoded by an enhanced R-GAT for basic flow generation (BFGen), and further supports branch point prediction through BPP and branch-conditioned alternative flow generation through AFGen. Evaluations on 13 public and 7 industrial datasets show that FlowGen consistently outperforms competitive baselines in all three core components. In particular, BFGen improves over the best baseline by 14% in Precision, 7-25% in Recall, 11-30% in F1, and 10-19% in AUC; BPP improves Precision by 30-110%, Recall by 33-91%, and F1 by 32-117%; AFGen improves Precision by 8-23%, F1 by 5-18%, and AUC by 0.6-2.5%. Moreover, we validate the effectiveness of the LLM-based SIP module and the attention preservation factor in BFGen, analyze the impact of requirement completeness on BFGen, and examine how different scopes of branch-related context affect AFGen.

</details>


### [118] [A Study of the Reliability of Agentic AI-Generated Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18298)
*Ayesha Shafique,Barton P. MIller,Elisa R. Heymann*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过对比AI智能体生成的10个Linux工具程序与人工编写的高质量版本，采用模糊随机测试（黑盒生成式与AFL++灰盒变异式）评估其可靠性，发现AI生成代码在可靠性上通常不逊于甚至优于人工版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体驱动的软件开发被宣称能加快完成速度、提升程序员效率并生成更可靠的代码，但这些说法缺乏客观验证，本文旨在以客观方法检验这些主张。

Method: 采用三方面实践：（1）应用典型最佳实践的智能体AI开发工作流；（2）以10个知名的、发行质量级的人工编写Linux工具程序为对比对象，提供明确的地面真值；（3）以广泛使用的模糊随机测试作为可靠性度量，结合经典黑盒生成式测试与现代基于覆盖率引导的灰盒变异测试（AFL++）。

Result: AI生成的工具程序版本通常与最新人工版本一样可靠，甚至往往更可靠；AI版本虽存在一些失败，但比标准代码库中的失败更少见。AI生成代码较少出现内存错误（如缓冲区溢出），但更易出现挂起（如无限循环）。

Conclusion: 使用智能体AI生成健壮可靠的软件需要谨慎实践与人类监督，代码质量高度依赖所用提示与技能以及人类指导者的应对方式；此外，智能体AI工作流（含提示与技能）可成为代码的规范说明，有助于软件的可持续性与成本效益。

Abstract: Agentic-AI based software development offers the promise of faster completion of the software, greater programmer efficiency, and more reliable code. The question is how can we verify these claims in an objective way? In this project, we attempted to answer this question based on three practices. First, we applied a typical best-practices agentic AI workflow for software development. Second, our target programs were ten well-known, release-quality human-written Linux utility programs so that we could compare the AI-generated code against a concrete ground truth. Third, we based our measure of reliability on a widely used testing technique, fuzz random testing. For this testing, we used both classic black box, generational testing and more modern coverage guided (gray box, mutational) testing using AFL++. We found that the AI-generated versions of the utility programs were typically as reliable - often more reliable - than the latest human-generated versions of these programs. While the AI-generated versions did have some failures, they were less common than the code from the standard repositories. Interestingly, the AI-generated code was less likely to have failures such as memory errors (such as buffer overflows) but more likely to have hangs such as infinite loops. In addition, we verified that generating robust and reliable software using agentic AI requires careful practice and human supervision. The quality of the code is highly dependent on the prompts and skills used, and how the human directing the process responds. We also demonstrated that using agentic AI workflow for software development (with its prompts and skills) can become a specification of the code that leads to cost-effective sustainability of the software.

</details>


### [119] [A Study on the Impact of Natural Language Differences in Prompts on Automatic Code Generation Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18311)
*Haruka Tokumasu,Masanari Kondo,Alexander Serebrenik,Dong Wang,Kei Koyanagi,Kotaro Noguchi,Naoyasu Ubayashi,Yasutaka Kamei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究自然语言（英语、日语、中文）对LLM代码生成性能的影响（语言偏置），并在AtCoder、LeetCode、BigCodeBench上评估翻译作为缓解策略的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大量研究探索了基于LLM的代码生成，但输入提示中自然语言的影响（语言偏置）尚未被探究。本研究旨在量化输入提示语言对代码生成性能的影响，并评估缓解语言偏置的策略。

Method: 在AtCoder、LeetCode和BigCodeBench上评估代码生成准确率。每个问题以英语、日语和中文三种语言呈现。使用7个LLM（GPT-4o、o3-mini、DeepSeek-V3.2、Llama-3、Qwen2.5-Coder-14B、Qwen2.5-Coder-0.5B和GitHub Copilot），以准确率（生成代码通过全部测试用例的问题数量）为指标，比较翻译前后的准确率以评估翻译作为缓解策略的有效性。

Result: 问题陈述的自然语言会影响基于LLM的代码生成性能。各数据集官方支持的语言取得最高中位准确率。翻译提升了准确率，但其有效性在不同数据集和模型类型间并不一致。AtCoder包含特别高比例的叙事风格问题陈述和更长的问题陈述。

Conclusion: 自然语言显著影响LLM代码生成的准确率。翻译可在某些情况下缓解语言偏置，但其有效性取决于数据集和模型类型。此外，输入提示的叙事特征和上下文长度是与语言偏置及翻译缓解策略有效性相关的重要因素。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance in automatic code generation tasks, thereby encouraging new research in this area. Although numerous studies have explored LLM-based code generation, the impact of the natural language in input prompts remains unexplored (language bias). This study aims to (1) quantify how the natural language of input prompts influences LLM-based code generation performance and (2) evaluate a mitigation strategy to reduce language bias in code generation. We assess code generation Accuracy on AtCoder, LeetCode, and BigCodeBench. To quantify the language bias on code generation, each problem is presented in English, Japanese, and Chinese. We use seven LLMs (GPT-4o, o3-mini, DeepSeek-V3.2, Llama-3, Qwen2.5-Coder-14B, Qwen2.5-Coder-0.5B, and GitHub Copilot) and assess their performance in terms of Accuracy (the number of problems for which generated code passes all test cases). We compare Accuracy before and after translation to evaluate the effectiveness of translation as a mitigation strategy. We observed that the natural language of problem statements affects LLM-based code generation performance. Specifically, the languages officially supported by each dataset achieved the highest median Accuracy. Also, translation improved Accuracy, but its effectiveness was not consistent across datasets and model types. We found that AtCoder contained a particularly high proportion of narrative-style problem statements and longer problem statements. Natural language significantly affects LLM code generation accuracy. Translation can mitigate language bias in some settings, but its effectiveness depends on the dataset and model type. Furthermore, the narrative aspects and context length of input prompts are important factors related to language bias and the effectiveness of translation as a mitigation strategy.

</details>


### [120] [From Component Snapshots to Lifecycle Traces: Agent-Based Software Composition Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18391)
*Chaofan Li,Zhengduo Xue,Chengxiang Li,Yutao Hu,Yueming Wu,Deqing Zou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 SCA-Agent，一种基于智能体的生命周期感知软件成分分析（SCA）方法，可在 Code、Build、Release、Deploy、Runtime 五个阶段重建有证据支撑的组件生命周期轨迹，在 105 个真实项目中取得最高检测 F1，并将漏洞暴露评估 F1 提升至 96.69%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件供应链安全需要准确识别第三方组件并理解其从开发到执行的演化过程。现有 SCA 方法仅分析清单文件、构建环境、发布产物、容器或运行时状态，通常只给出某一阶段的软件成分视图。由于依赖在生命周期各阶段会被解析、移除、重新打包和转换，单一快照无法同时刻画组件的来源与最终去向；即便组合多个阶段的快照，阶段间的关联关系仍未被解决。

Method: 提出 SCA-Agent，一种基于智能体（agent-based）的生命周期感知 SCA 方法。它自适应地探索项目特定的分析路径，采集各阶段证据，并跨阶段关联观测结果，从而恢复组件身份、版本、引入路径、传播关系及最终生命周期状态，构建覆盖 Code、Build、Release、Deploy、Runtime 的组件生命周期轨迹。

Result: 在来自 Java、JavaScript 和 Python 生态的 105 个真实项目上进行评估。SCA-Agent 在所有生命周期阶段和生态系统中均取得最高的组件检测 F1。在漏洞暴露评估中，其 F1 达到 96.69%，比最佳传统 SCA 工具高出 18.76 个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，生命周期感知的 SCA 能够支持可追溯的组件来源分析，并实现更准确的软件供应链风险评估。

Abstract: Software supply-chain security requires accurate identification of third-party components and an understanding of how they evolve from development to execution. Existing software composition analysis (SCA) approaches examine manifests, build environments, release artifacts, containers, or runtime states, but typically produce only stage-specific views of software composition. As dependencies are resolved, removed, repackaged, and transformed across lifecycle stages, a single snapshot cannot capture both where a component originates and where it ultimately ends up. Combining snapshots from multiple stages still leaves their cross-stage relationships unresolved. We present SCA-Agent, an agent-based approach to lifecycle-aware SCA that reconstructs evidence-backed component lifecycle traces across Code, Build, Release, Deploy, and Runtime. SCA-Agent adaptively explores project-specific analysis paths, gathers stage-specific evidence, and correlates observations across stages to recover component identities, versions, introduction paths, propagation relationships, and final lifecycle states. We evaluate SCA-Agent on 105 real-world projects from the Java, JavaScript, and Python ecosystems. SCA-Agent achieves the highest component detection F1 across all lifecycle stages and ecosystems. For vulnerability exposure assessment, it reaches an F1 score of 96.69%, exceeding the best traditional SCA tool by 18.76 percentage points. These results show that lifecycle-aware SCA supports traceable component provenance and more accurate software supply-chain risk assessment.

</details>


### [121] [Evaluating the Health of Open-Source Smart City Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18801)
*Rodrigo Bravo Simões,Fernando Brito e Abreu,Vasco Amaral*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文从"开源健康度（OSS health）"视角评估多个开源智慧城市平台（SCP），以帮助技术资源有限的城市IT团队在采纳前做出决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源智慧城市平台（SCP）免费且有助于数字主权、避免厂商锁定，适合预算有限的城市；但缺乏对开源的支持妨碍了其被采纳，因此需要一套评估其生态系统健康度的方法来辅助选型。

Method: 引入"OSS health（开源健康度）"概念，涵盖开发者与用户生态的协调性、安装与用户手册的可得性、代码无漏洞且文档化、测试套件及其他制品等特征，并据此评估不同的开源SCP。

Result: 论文对多个开源智慧城市平台进行了基于OSS health特征的评估（摘要未给出具体量化结果）。

Conclusion: OSS health是城市采纳开源SCP的重要决策因素；通过多维度健康度评估可帮助技术资源匮乏的城市IT团队甄别适合的平台。

Abstract: To manage the complexity of smart cities, a variety of smart city platforms (SCPs), both proprietary and open-source, have been proposed. Typically acting as middleware between IoT devices at a lower layer and smart services at a higher layer, these platforms simplify the management of smart cities by reifying a variety of requirements common to different municipalities. Open-source software (OSS) is typically free of charge, making open-source SCPs an attractive option for municipalities with smaller budgets that still wish to improve efficiency and quality of life for residents and visitors. Furthermore, using an OSS SCP promotes digital sovereignty, since municipalities can control where the data will reside and will be less prone to vendor lock-in.
  A lack of support for OSS hinders its adoption by municipalities' IT teams, often with scarce technical resources. An active (aka "healthy") ecosystem, including well-orchestrated developers and users, the availability of installation and user manuals, non-vulnerable and documented code, test batteries, and other artifacts, is an important decision factor before adopting an OSS SCP. In this paper, we evaluate different open-source SCPs using the concept of "OSS health", covering several of the aforementioned characteristics.

</details>


### [122] [ProgramDistill: From Interactive Web Apps to Verifiable Reference-Guided SWE Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18805)
*Jeonghye Kim,Minseon Kim,Young Jin Kim,Matheus Pereira,Marc-Alexandre Côté,Alessandro Sordoni,Xingdi Yuan,Zhengyan Shi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ProgramDistill 是一个通过功能分解与重放验证构建的编程智能体评测基准，用于评估智能体从可运行参考应用中推断并复现功能的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编程智能体评测通常通过 issue 或指令显式指定目标行为，但在实际 Web 开发中，智能体往往需要从可工作的软件中推断行为，并在不完整的应用中加以实现，这一能力缺乏专门的评测手段。

Method: 作者提出 ProgramDistill，将应用分解为不同粒度的功能，每个功能关联可通过其 gold patch 重放的行为。构建流程 mine-craft-patch 在 26 个应用上自动挖掘出 1,975 个经重放验证的行为，并构建 4,063 个任务，全程无需人工干预。

Result: 在九个前沿编程智能体上评测，GPT-6 Astra 与 Claude Opus 5 在全应用重建的累积工作流上成功率分别为 49.2% 和 28.8%；在部分应用重建中，当恢复深度从 1 增加到 8 时，成功率分别从 100% 降至 64.0%、从 96% 降至 32%。

Conclusion: ProgramDistill 提供了一个可扩展、难度可控的基准，可用于评测与诊断编程智能体，并为未来基于课程的学习训练奠定了自然基础。

Abstract: Coding agents are typically evaluated with desired behavior specified through issues or instructions. In practical web development, however, agents may need to infer behavior from working software and implement it in an incomplete application. We introduce ProgramDistill, a benchmark evaluating coding agents on features discovered through interaction with fully functional reference applications. We build ProgramDistill by factorizing applications into features of different granularities, each associated with replayable behaviors executable via its gold patch. Our pipeline, mine-craft-patch, discovers 1,975 replay-verified behaviors across 26 applications and constructs 4,063 tasks without human intervention. Across nine frontier coding agents, GPT-6 Astra and Claude Opus 5 achieve 49.2% and 28.8% success on cumulative workflows in full-application reconstruction. In partial-application reconstruction, success falls from 100% to 64.0% and from 96% to 32% as restoration depth increases from 1 to 8. ProgramDistill thus provides a scalable benchmark with controlled difficulty for evaluating and diagnosing coding agents, and a natural basis for future curriculum-based training.

</details>


### [123] [Removing Noise or Introducing Bias? The Hidden Cost of MSR Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18953)
*Mohit Kaushik,Jyoti Bawa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究分析SEART平台157万个仓库，揭示MSR研究中常用过滤阈值导致的维护偏差、生态偏差和关系偏差，建议采用分层抽样并优化噪声检测标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GitHub等OSS平台是MSR研究的主要数据源，但用户群体多样（从学生到开发者），并非所有仓库都是真实工程项目。研究者常用多种标准过滤噪声，这些标准可能从根本上改变样本人口统计特征，因此需要揭示这些任意阈值或标准带来的隐藏代价与偏差。

Method: 分析SEART平台的157万个仓库，依据MSR研究中常用的各类阈值构建多个数据集，对比过滤前后样本特征的差异，考察过滤过程对项目弃用状况、生态系统与治理风格分布以及变量间关系的影响。

Result: 识别出过滤过程中的维护偏差：掩盖了OSS项目真实的弃用比例（73.42%）；过滤策略偏向某些生态系统和治理风格；抽样策略还扭曲了变量之间的关系，产生关系偏差。

Conclusion: 为避免上述偏差，研究者应转向分层抽样（stratified sampling），并优化噪声检测标准（refining the criteria for noise detection）。

Abstract: OSS platforms like GitHub serve as a primary data source for MSR research. As the platform is widely used by different users, spanning from student to developer, not all repositories are actual engineered projects. Therefore, to avoid such noise, researchers often apply several criteria, which may fundamentally change the sample demographics. To understand such biases, this study aims to uncover the hidden cost originating from these arbitrary thresholds or criteria. We analyzed 1.57 million repositories from the SEART platform and constructed several datasets from the thresholds often applied in MSR research. We identify the maintenance bias in these filtering processes, which masks the true abandonment (73.42%) realities of OSS projects. Also, these strategies favor some ecosystems and governance styles. Moreover, the sampling strategy also distorts the relationship between variables, suffering from relational biases. Therefore, to avoid such biases, the researchers should shift towards stratified sampling and refining the criteria for noise detection.

</details>


### [124] [Code Consistency Preference Optimization Verification for Language Model Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19002)
*Yunlong Tan,Mingqiao Mo,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于执行一致性偏好优化的方法，通过构建带依赖图的科学推理数据，提升大模型数学与科学推理能力，并形成CCPO模型族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Bradley-Terry奖励模型的偏好优化方法无法捕捉科学任务所需的逻辑依赖与执行一致性，因此需要引入执行验证与依赖感知的优化机制。

Method: 使用UltraFeedback提示构建科学推理数据集（含模型生成、验证与一致性结果）；提取推理步骤表达式、前置条件与可推导关系以构建依赖图，计算执行一致性分数；将分数附加于每步形成配对训练数据，并对Llama-3-8B与DeepSeekMath-7B进行微调；扩展Scientific Feasibility Control框架。

Result: MATH提升17.0%，GSM8K提升15.1%；PhyX多模态物理推理达到50.1%准确率，超过DeepSeek-R1（49.8%）与OpenAI o3-mini（48.2%）；在alpha=0.10下科学有效性覆盖率达91.7%，科学定律违背减少73%。

Conclusion: 执行一致性偏好优化与依赖图建模可显著增强大模型的数学与科学推理表现，并形成CCPO模型族。

Abstract: Execution-based verification enhances large language models' mathematical reasoning through computational soundness and dependency-aware filtering. However, prior preference optimization methods relying on Bradley-Terry reward models fail to capture the logical dependencies and execution consistency needed for scientific tasks. We propose a method that generates computationally sound solutions with dependency graphs for execution-consistent preference optimization. We first build a scientific reasoning dataset using UltraFeedback prompts, model generations, verification, and consistency results. Then we extract reasoning step expressions, prerequisites, and derivability relationships to construct dependency graphs and compute execution consistency scores. These scores are appended to each step, creating paired training data. Fine-tuning Llama-3-8B and DeepSeekMath-7B yields significant gains: +17.0% on MATH and +15.1% on GSM8K. Extending our Scientific Feasibility Control framework achieves 50.1% accuracy on PhyX multimodal physics reasoning, surpassing DeepSeek-R1 (49.8%) and OpenAI o3-mini (48.2%), with 91.7% scientific validity coverage at alpha=0.10 and 73% fewer scientific law violations, resulting in the CCPO model family.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Scaling Articulated Rationales for MLLM-based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17639)
*Haoke Xiao,Yueyang Liu,Yuhui Zhang,Xiang Chen,Yufei Liu,Jia Xu,Yalong Guan,Xiaolan Zhu,Xiaoyu Zhang,Shijun Wang,Shuang Yang,Zijie Meng,Zejian Zhang,Ruochen Yang,Xiangyu Wu,Tingting Gao,Han Li,Lantao Hu,Cheng Luo,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出SARA工业框架，将稀疏的用户自然语言偏好解释(AUR)转化为可扩展的推荐信号，含数据引擎、SARA-7B生成模型与SARA-Ranker排序模型，并在快手线上部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推荐系统依赖点击、观看时长等隐式行为推断偏好，只能反映用户"做了什么"，无法揭示"为什么喜欢或不喜欢"。AUR（用户自然语言偏好解释）具备极性与理由层面的信息价值，但天然稀疏、质量参差、仅覆盖少量物品，难以在工业系统中直接使用。

Method: SARA包含三部分：(1) 数据引擎：从2.4亿快手直播用户中诱导并整理AUR，构建质量受控、以作者为中心的SARA-HQ数据集；(2) 通过大规模SFT与Quality-Refining DPO将通用MLLM对齐为SARA-7B，把理由生成从86,564个有AUR覆盖的作者扩展至全部1000万作者空间；(3) SARA-Ranker通过理由感知交互建模与拒绝记忆建模，将生成的正负理由整合进生产排序。

Result: 离线评估、人工校准与在线A/B测试表明：SARA-7B生成的理由比强MLLM基线更具特异性、极性一致性与可溯源性；SARA-Ranker提升了用户参与度并降低了负反馈。系统以每日刷新方式上线运行超过30天。

Conclusion: SARA确立了AUR作为工业推荐系统中实用的一等文本信号，为解决隐式行为信号"只知其然"的局限提供了可规模化落地的方案。

Abstract: Modern recommendation systems largely infer user preferences from implicit behaviors such as clicks, watch time, and negative feedback, but these signals reveal what users do rather than why they like or dislike content. This work studies articulated user rationales (AURs), i.e., users' natural-language explanations of their preferences, as a new class of polarity-aware and reason-level textual signals for recommendation. Despite their potential value, AURs are difficult to use in industrial systems because they are naturally sparse, often low-quality, and only cover a small fraction of items. We present SARA (Scaling Articulated Rationales), an industrial framework that turns sparse AURs into scalable recommendation signals. SARA first builds a data engine that elicits and curates AURs from 240M Kuaishou Live users, producing SARA-HQ, a quality-controlled and author-centric rationale dataset. It then aligns a general-purpose MLLM into SARA-7B through large-scale SFT and Quality-Refining DPO, extending rationale generation from 86,564 AUR-covered authors to the full 10M-author space. Finally, SARA-Ranker integrates the generated positive and negative rationales into production ranking via rationale-aware interaction modeling and rejection-memory modeling. Extensive offline evaluation, human calibration, and online A/B tests show that SARA-7B generates more specific, polarity-consistent, and grounded rationales than strong MLLM baselines, while SARA-Ranker improves engagement and reduces negative feedback in production. Deployed with daily refresh for over 30 days, SARA establishes articulated rationales as a practical, first-class textual signal for industrial recommendation systems.

</details>


### [126] [One Size Does Not Fit All! Dynamic Retriever and Generator Selection for RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17709)
*Neeraj Anand,Payel Santra,Partha Basuchowdhuri,Debasis Ganguly,Sumit Bhatia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文系统分析了RAG中检索器与生成器复杂度的交互效应，发现更强检索收益更大但存在递减与非单调回报，进而提出查询自适应的DRAG框架（含免训练的DRAG_QPP与有监督的DRAG_SFT），在多个LLM与QA基准上实现更优的效果-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统对不同查询采用固定的检索器与生成器配置，忽略了查询复杂度与信息需求的差异，导致计算资源分配低效；虽已有研究分别探讨检索自适应与生成自适应，但二者对端到端RAG性能的联合影响仍缺乏系统研究。

Method: 首先在事实型与多跳QA（含bridge与composition推理）上系统分析检索器与生成器复杂度的交互作用；随后提出查询自适应框架DRAG：DRAG_QPP为免训练路由方法，利用查询性能预测（QPP）信号指导检索器选择，并基于检索上下文的困惑度度量指导生成器选择；DRAG_SFT为有监督路由方法，微调LLM以联合预测检索器-生成器配置。

Result: 分析表明更强检索通常比增加生成开销带来更大收益，但二者均呈现递减且非单调的回报，高复杂度配置并非对所有查询都更优。在三个LLM家族与四个QA基准上，DRAG_QPP性能与强静态RAG基线相当，同时显著降低推理延迟；DRAG_SFT则在有效性上持续优于静态与免训练自适应基线。

Conclusion: 联合自适应地选择检索器与生成器配置，相比静态RAG流水线能够获得更优的效果-效率权衡，验证了检索与生成协同适配的价值。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems typically employ fixed retriever and generator configurations across queries, despite substantial differences in query complexity and information needs, leading to inefficient allocation of computational resources. While retrieval and generation adaptivity have been studied independently, their joint effect on end-to-end RAG performance remains underexplored. We systematically analyze how retriever and generator complexity interacts across factoid and multi-hop question answering (QA), including bridge and composition reasoning tasks. Our analysis shows that stronger retrieval generally yields larger gains than increased generation effort, but both exhibit diminishing and non-monotonic returns, indicating that higher-complexity configurations are not uniformly better across queries. Motivated by these findings, we introduce DRAG, a query-adaptive framework for selecting retriever-generator configurations. We first propose DRAG$_\text{QPP}$, a training-free routing approach that uses Query Performance Prediction (QPP) signals to guide retriever selection and perplexity-based measures over retrieved context to guide generator selection. We further introduce DRAG$_\text{SFT}$, a supervised routing approach that fine-tunes an LLM to jointly predict retriever-generator configurations. Across three LLM families and four QA benchmarks, \qpprag~achieves performance comparable to strong static RAG baselines while substantially reducing inference latency, whereas DRAG$_\text{SFT}$ consistently improves effectiveness over static and training-free adaptive baselines. Overall, DRAG demonstrates that jointly adapting retrieval and generation achieves a more favorable effectiveness-efficiency trade-off than static RAG pipelines.

</details>


### [127] [DUPAR: Dual-Path Conversational Retrieval via Speech Retriever with Cross-Turn Evidence Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18042)
*Yuanjun Li,Yiwen Liu,Dapeng Li,Zhiwei Xu,Bin Zhang,Shengtao Zhang,Rong Shen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 DUPAR 对话检索框架，采用快慢双路径：快路径用任务适配音频编码器对齐冻结的 BGE-M3 文本嵌入检索跨轮证据缓存，置信不足时慢路径融合音频与 ASR 转写嵌入做全索引检索，并通过一跳图扩展刷新下一轮缓存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于外部知识的语音助手通常先经 ASR 转写再检索文本知识库，该级联方式增加延迟并传播识别错误；而直接语音检索则面临跨模态对齐不准的问题。

Method: DUPAR 包含快慢两条互补路径。快路径：任务适配的音频编码器与冻结的 BGE-M3 文本嵌入对齐，检索跨轮证据缓存。慢路径：当缓存置信度不足时，融合音频与 ASR 转写嵌入进行全索引检索。所选证据通过一跳图扩展刷新下一轮证据缓存。

Result: 在领域知识库上，训练后的音频编码器在干净语音下接近文本检索精度，查询端较 ASR+文本编码器提速 3.75 倍；噪声基准上平均 Recall@10 从 0.771 提升至 0.875，跨合成说话风格整体 Recall@1 提升 4.2 个百分点。相比全索引音频检索，当前一轮检索到正确证据且后续目标为一跳相邻文本块时，跨轮证据缓存显著降低检索错误。

Conclusion: 快慢双路径与跨轮证据缓存的结合可有效缓解级联延迟与跨模态错配问题，在保持检索精度的同时提升速度，并借助图扩展利用会话上下文降低后续轮次检索错误。

Abstract: Voice assistants grounded in external knowledge typically use automatic speech recognition (ASR) to transcribe speech queries before retrieving evidence from textual knowledge bases. This cascade adds latency and propagates recognition errors, whereas direct speech retrieval is vulnerable to cross-modal misalignment. To address these limitations, we propose DUPAR, a conversational retrieval framework with complementary slow and fast paths. The fast path uses a task-adapted audio encoder aligned with frozen BGE-M3 text embeddings to search a cross-turn evidence cache. When cache confidence is insufficient, the slow path fuses full-index retrieval using audio and ASR-transcript embeddings, and the selected evidence refreshes the next-turn evidence cache through one-hop graph expansion. On a domain-specific knowledge base, our trained audio encoder approaches text-retrieval accuracy on clean speech with a 3.75$\times$ query-side speedup over ASR + Text Encoder. It raises average Recall@10 from 0.771 to 0.875 on the noise benchmark and improves overall Recall@1 by 4.2 percentage points across synthesized speaking styles. Compared with full-index audio retrieval, cross-turn evidence caching significantly reduces retrieval errors when the previous turn retrieves correct evidence and the follow-up targets a one-hop neighboring chunk.

</details>


### [128] [PageRecall: Measuring Page Selection in Literature-Grounded Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18154)
*Aaditya Chauhan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GroundLM 系统通过"检索整篇论文而非选择页面"的策略，解决了 LitTraceQA 中文档级证据定位的检索瓶颈，最终在测试集上取得 0.762 论文 F1、0.441 证据 F1 与 0.920 选择题准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文指出当前证据接地（evidence grounding）流程的瓶颈在检索环节而非阅读环节：页面选择器仅约一半时间能把金标页面送入模型，而模型一旦拿到正确页面，定位证据的准确率极高。这一不对称说明损失主要来自"正确页面从未被展示"，而非模型阅读能力不足。

Method: 1) 在 27,487 篇论文池中检索相关论文；2) 分析页面选择器与证据定位器的失败模式；3) 由于每篇检索到的论文可完整放入模型上下文，改为向模型展示整篇论文，页面排序仅作为超长论文的兜底机制；4) 对以位置而非内容指代目标的题目，通过解析参考文献为可寻址列表来解题；5) 发布验证性 harness，用已提交的工件核验论文核心主张。

Result: 页面选择器的金标页面召回率仅 52.6%；但给定正确页面时，证据定位器 48 次中有 45 次引用正确（94%）。页面缺失时，45 个案例中 14 次返回空、24 次返回错误页面、7 次给出正确页面，即静默失败约为显式失败的近两倍。改为整篇输入后，在可解析论文上金标页面召回率达 100%。最终系统在留出测试集上取得 0.762 论文 F1、0.441 证据 F1、0.920 选择题准确率。

Conclusion: 证据接地的瓶颈在于检索而非阅读；放弃页面选择、改为整篇论文输入是可解析场景下的有效修正。位置型问题应通过参考文献解析而非检索解决。由于系统依赖无种子控制的闭源模型，作者发布 harness 以基于已提交工件复核核心结论。

Abstract: We describe our system for LitTraceQA (GroundLM @ EMNLP 2026): given a research question, retrieve the relevant papers from a pool of 27,487, cite the page and the table or figure where the answer lives, and answer in a requested format. Our main finding is that evidence grounding is limited by retrieval, not by reading. The page selector put the annotator's page, which we call the gold page, in front of the model that locates evidence only about half the time (52.6% gold-page recall), while that model, given the page, cited the right one in 45 of the 48 locators it emitted (94%). When the page was missing it rarely said so: of 45 such cases it returned nothing 14 times, a wrong page 24 times, and a correct page 7 times, so the pipeline failed quietly almost twice as often as it failed visibly. Since the failure was that the right page was never shown, the fix is to stop choosing: each retrieved paper fits in the model's context, so we show it whole. Page ranking survives only as a fallback inside papers too long to fit, which no test-split paper was, and gold-page recall reaches 100% on the papers we can parse. Separately, questions that identify their target by position rather than content, such as "the first author of the 24th reference", are served by parsing rather than retrieval: we resolve the bibliography into an addressable list, which also supplies identifiers the evidence metric scores. The final system scores 0.762 paper $F_1$, 0.441 evidence $F_1$ and 0.920 multiple-choice accuracy on the held-out test split. Because the pipeline depends on a closed model without seed control, we release a harness that verifies the paper's central claims against committed artifacts.

</details>


### [129] [Single-Token Expected-Value Scoring for Cold-Start Candidate Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18188)
*Qihang Wang,Jinwei Tan,Mengyuan Shi,Mayank Sharma,Shuai Zhao,Fuxian Li,Ryan Yan,Alexander P. Kreuzer,Mohit Jain,Dheeraj Toshniwal,Manoj Seethamsetty*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出"单token期望值打分"排序原语，将候选人-职位相关性建模为对{1,...,5}等级token的有序分类，用第一token概率分布的期望作为相关性分数，并以MSE+交叉熵混合有序回归损失微调小语言模型，在离线、仿真和在线实验中均优于启发式基线与零样本LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助招聘虽能减轻人工筛选负担，但将语言模型部署为生产排序器仍面临困境：零样本LLM打分不稳定、非确定性且排序精度低；传统深度神经排序器需要数百万条交互日志，而低流量的小众招聘平台无法产生这样的数据。平台实际拥有的是几十万条有序相关性标签——按排序器训练标准看较少，但当预训练语言模型已编码任务所需的通用世界知识时则足够。

Method: 提出单token期望值打分：将候选人-职位相关性转化为对等级token{1,...,5}的有序分类，以第一token概率分布的期望作为相关性分数。由于分数来自单步解码而非开放式生成，它是模型logits的确定性函数，无需输出解析且延迟低。为仅凭该监督信号学习异构招聘标准的非线性相互依赖关系，用混合有序回归损失微调小语言模型（SLM），该损失结合保持有序距离的MSE项与锐化类别边界的分类交叉熵项。

Result: 沿求职者相关性和雇主相关性两个维度，用NDCG@10和低相关率评估。离线实验中，微调模型优于启发式基线和零样本LLM。端到端仿真显示同方向且幅度更大（求职者NDCG@10 +54.2%，低相关率 -46.7%）。在线实验使雇主低相关率降低27.3%，雇主留存率提升7.07%。

Conclusion: 单token期望值打分配合混合有序回归损失微调的SLM，能够在低流量、小众招聘场景下以少量有序标签实现确定性、低延迟且有效的候选-职位相关性排序，离线、仿真与在线实验结果一致表明其优于启发式基线和零样本LLM。

Abstract: AI-assisted sourcing streamlines candidate review, reducing the administrative burden of manual screening for recruiters. However, deploying language models as production rankers remains challenging. Zero-shot Large Language Models (LLMs) may produce unstable, non-deterministic scores and rank less accurately, while conventional deep neural rankers require millions of logged interactions that a low-traffic, niche sourcing platform does not produce. What is available instead is a few hundred thousand ordinal relevance labels -- small by ranker-training standards, but sufficient when a pretrained language model already encodes the general world knowledge the task depends on.
  We present single-token expected-value scoring, a ranking primitive that casts candidate-job relevance as an ordinal classification over the grade tokens {1, ..., 5} and reads the relevance score as the expectation of the first-token probability distribution. Because the score comes from a single decoding step rather than open-ended generation, it is a deterministic function of the model's logits, requires no output parsing, and serves at low latency. To learn the non-linear interdependencies of heterogeneous hiring criteria from this supervision alone, we fine-tune a Small Language Model (SLM) with a hybrid ordinal regression loss combining a Mean Squared Error term, which preserves ordinal distance, with a categorical Cross-Entropy term, which sharpens class boundaries.
  We evaluate along two dimensions -- Jobseeker Relevance and Employer Relevance -- using NDCG@10 and low relevance rate. Offline, our fine-tuned model outperforms a heuristic baseline and zero-shot LLMs. An end-to-end simulation shows the same direction at larger magnitude (+54.2% Jobseeker NDCG@10, -46.7% low relevance rate), and a live online experiment reduces employer low-relevance by 27.3% and raises employer keep rate by 7.07%.

</details>


### [130] [Quanta: A Self-Contained Python Library for Hybrid Retrieval over Quantised Embeddings, Lexical Indexes, and Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18248)
*Ioannis E. Livieris*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出开源Python库Quanta,将4比特量化稠密向量检索、BM25全文检索与知识图谱遍历统一到单一检索API之下,并采用加权倒数排名融合与"图谱仅作候选扩展器"两项设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有先进RAG流水线通常由3-4个独立系统拼装而成(近似最近邻索引、全文搜索引擎、图数据库、关系型文档存储)。每个系统都带来独立的部署面、配置模型与故障模式,且连接它们的集成逻辑在每个项目中都要重写,造成工程重复与维护负担。

Method: Quanta在单一检索API后统一三种检索能力:(1)4比特量化嵌入上的稠密向量搜索;(2)BM25全文检索;(3)知识图谱遍历。其两项关键设计承诺为:第一,使用加权倒数排名融合(weighted reciprocal rank fusion)组合多路信号,而非将异质分数归一化到统一区间——论文认为后者是病态的,因为此类归一化依赖查询;第二,将图谱定位为候选扩展器而非相关性打分器,即遍历用于拓宽候选池,新增文档在标识符允许列表(allowlist)约束下由稠密索引重新打分,从而由结构邻接决定"考虑哪些文档",由内容证据决定"如何排序"。

Result: 摘要未给出量化实验结果;主要产出为一个开源Python库及其统一检索API与上述融合、扩展机制的设计实现。

Conclusion: Quanta通过单一API整合稠密、稀疏与图结构三种检索范式,规避了多系统拼接的集成成本;其方法学贡献在于提出基于秩而非分数的融合策略,以及"图谱管候选、内容管排序"的职责分离原则。

Abstract: An advanced retrieval-augmented generation pipeline is typically assembled from three or four independently operated systems: an approximate nearest-neighbour index, a full-text search engine, a graph database, and a relational document store. Each contributes its own deployment surface, configuration model, and failure modes, and the integration logic that binds them is written anew in every project. In this work, we present \textsc{Quanta}, an open-source Python library, which unifies dense vector search over 4-bit quantised embeddings, BM25 full-text retrieval, and knowledge-graph traversal behind a single retrieval API. Quanta makes two design commitments, which distinguish it from existing hybrid retrieval stacks. First, signals are combined by \emph{weighted reciprocal rank fusion} rather than by normalising heterogeneous scores onto a shared range, which we argue is ill-posed because such normalisations are query-dependent. Second, the graph is a \emph{candidate expander and not a relevance scorer}: traversal widens the candidate pool, and the newly admitted documents are re-scored by the dense indexes under an identifier allowlist, so structural adjacency determines what is considered while content evidence determines how it ranks.

</details>


### [131] [One-Step Retrieval Framework for Real-Time Sponsored Search Ads Using Hierarchical Text Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18296)
*Tongtong Liu,Renyu Zhang,Jiayu Ding,Hongchao Guo,Xintao Yang,He Wei,Zhaoyu Li,Haiyang Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出ANGLE框架，用LLM生成层次化文本表征替代离散SID，将检索、相关性、排序统一在单一LLM内，在真实搜索广告场景中实现消费+1.81%、GMV+2.16%，离线指标HR/ACR全面超越七个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多阶段级联架构各模块独立优化，目标不一致且会过早淘汰高潜候选；现有LLM生成式方法依赖离散语义标识符（SID），SID未经基础LLM学习，需在SFT中记忆大量SID到广告的映射，导致对未见广告泛化差、维护更新成本高；SID与广告的一对一映射使解码效率低；且仅依赖小奖励模型（如pctr）评估相关性与排序，限制LLM对广告商业价值的全面评估。

Method: ANGLE（A uNified Generation-discriminative-ranking reaL-time rEtrieval）框架：使用LLM生成的层次化文本表征，包含提供高层概览的商业意图（commercial intent）与提供细粒度细节的广告摘要（ad abstract）；将检索、相关性判断与排序直接集成到单个LLM中，利用LLM的完整能力实现精准高效的广告排序。

Result: 在真实搜索场景中应用，消费提升1.81%，商品交易总额（GMV）提升2.16%；离线评估中与七个基线对比，在HR、ACR等关键指标上均优于所有基线。

Conclusion: ANGLE通过层次化文本表征与生成-判别-排序一体化的单LLM架构，缓解了多阶段级联的目标不一致与SID方案的泛化、效率及维护问题，在在线商业指标与离线检索指标上均取得一致提升。

Abstract: Traditional retrieval systems typically use multi-stage cascading architectures (MCA), where each module is optimized independently, leading to inconsistent objectives and the premature elimination of high-potential candidates. Recent LLM-based generation methods offer end-to-end solutions but use discrete semantic identifiers (SIDs) to retrieve ads, which are not learned by the base LLM and require memorization of numerous SID-to-ad mappings during SFT, suffering from limited generalization to unseen ads, high maintenance and update costs. The one-to-one mapping between SIDs and advertisements leads to inefficient decoding. Moreover, these methods rely on a small reward model (e.g. pctr) for relevance and ranking, limiting the LLM's ability to fully assess ads' commercial value. To address these challenges, we propose A uNified Generation-discriminative-ranking reaL-time rEtrieval (ANGLE) framework. ANGLE uses LLM-generated hierarchical textual representations, which consist of commercial intent that provide high-level overviews and ad abstract that deliver fine-grained details. Additionally, ANGLE integrates retrieval, relevance, and ranking directly within a single LLM, enabling precise and efficient ranking of ads by leveraging the full capabilities of the LLM. We applied ANGLE to the real-world search scenarios, achieving a 1.81% increase in consumption and a 2.16% increase in gross merchandise volume (GMV). We also conducted offline evaluations of ANGLE and seven baselines, with ANGLE outperforming all across key metrics such as HR and ACR.

</details>


### [132] [Exploring LLMs and RAG for Plausible and Explainable Material Prediction of Vehicle Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18437)
*Frederik Wagner,Annerose Eichel,Sabine Schulte im Walde*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文探讨LLM能否在不经大量微调的情况下，准确预测并解释车辆部件（如刹车盘、喷油器）的可行材料，并比较生成式基线、单轮RAG与迭代式CoVe三种方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆部件材料选择属于专业领域任务，缺少金标准数据，且高质量、可合法获取的领域语料稀缺，因此需要评估LLM在此类任务中的实际能力及RAG的增益。

Method: 构建基于领域过滤Wikipedia语料的检索；对比三种方案：标准生成式LLM基线、单轮RAG、迭代式Chain-of-Verification（CoVe）变体；并开发支持结构化领域专家评估的网页标注工具。

Result: LLM生成结果显著优于既有工作，但所测试的RAG方法并未进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: RAG系统仍面临超参数优化、高质量且合法可访问的领域语料获取，以及专家评估实验设计等挑战。

Abstract: In this work, we explore whether LLMs can accurately predict and explain plausible materials for vehicle components such as brake discs or fuel injectors without requiring extensive fine-tuning. We test and evaluate three approaches: a standard generative LLM baseline, a single-pass Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) approach, and an iterative Chain-of-Verification (CoVe) variant. For retrieval, we rely on publicly available data using a domain-filtered Wikipedia corpus. Since no gold standard exists for this task, we develop a custom web-based annotation tool supporting crucial functions for structured domain expert evaluation. LLM-based generation substantially outperforms prior work, which is not further surpassed by the tested RAG approaches. Our results surface remaining challenges for RAG-based systems: hyperparameter optimization, the availability of high-quality, legally accessible domain corpora, and expert evaluation study design.

</details>


### [133] [SURF: Subtractive Updates for Recommender Forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18695)
*Filippo Betello,Antonio Purificato,Nicola Tonellotto,Fabrizio Silvestri*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SURF，一种用于序列推荐系统近似机器遗忘的三阶段轻量框架，通过定位删除项邻域、训练辅助模型并做分数相减实现遗忘，效果接近全量重训且成本极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户隐私需求与GDPR等法规要求使机器遗忘成为现代推荐系统的基本能力。但序列推荐系统依赖时序交互模式，遗忘挑战独特：现有方法要么需要计算代价高昂的全量重训，要么忽略用户行为的序列特性。

Method: SURF（Subtractive Updates for Recommender Forgetting）分三阶段：（i）在嵌入空间中识别待遗忘物品的邻域；（ii）在该紧凑的局部子集上训练辅助模型；（iii）推理时将辅助模型的分数从原始模型中相减。

Result: 在7个数据集上对比5个基线，SURF的遗忘有效性可与全量重训相媲美，同时大幅降低计算成本：NDCG@20最高提升32%，且仅需原重训基线约2%的时间预算。

Conclusion: SURF为序列推荐系统提供了一种高效、轻量的近似机器遗忘方案，兼顾遗忘效果与计算效率，并已开源代码以支持复现与后续研究。

Abstract: The increasing demand for user privacy and compliance with regulations such as GDPR has made machine unlearning a fundamental requirement for modern recommender systems. However, Sequential Recommender Systems (SRS) pose unique challenges for unlearning due to their reliance on temporal interaction patterns. Existing approaches either require computationally prohibitive full retraining or fail to account for the sequential nature of user behavior.
